<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机试验聚焦平均效应，掩盖了临床上有意义的治疗反应异质性；利用真实世界数据模拟临床试验并估计HTE，有望实现更精准高效的试验设计。

Method: 使用梅奥诊所电子健康记录（MCC）模拟DAPA-HF试验；采用Cox比例风险模型评估全因死亡率；利用Meta-S学习器估计HTE；通过基于决策树的阈值法定义患者亚组。

Result: 全队列模拟分析未显示达格列净显著生存获益（HR=1.681, p=0.1507）；HTE分层后，低HTE亚组显著获益（HR=0.203, p=0.0002），高HTE亚组显著有害（HR=6.680, p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: HTE引导的分层策略能够揭露被全队列分析掩盖的、临床上有意义的获益与有害治疗效应模式，为精准医学和个性化试验设计提供依据。

Abstract: Traditional randomized trials often obscure clinically meaningful heterogeneity in treatment response by focusing on average effects. Leveraging real-world data to emulate clinical trials and estimate heterogeneous treatment effects (HTEs) offers a promising path toward more precise and efficient trial design. In this study, we emulate the DAPA-HF trial using electronic health records from the Mayo Clinic Cloud (MCC) to investigate whether HTE-guided stratification can identify patient subgroups with distinct treatment responses to dapagliflozin versus placebo in patients with heart failure with reduced ejection fraction. All-cause mortality was evaluated using Cox proportional hazards models, with HTEs estimated using a Meta-S learner and subgroups defined using a decision tree-based thresholding approach. In the overall cohort of the emulation, no significant treatment difference was observed (HR, 1.681; 95% CI, 0.828-3.413; p = 0.1507). However, compared with the overall emulated cohort, in which dapagliflozin showed no statistically significant survival benefit, HTE-driven stratification identified subgroups with significant and directionally distinct treatment effects. The beneficial (low-HTE) subgroup showed a significant survival benefit from dapagliflozin (HR = 0.203, 95% CI, 0.087-0.476, p = 0.0002), whereas the harmful (high-HTE) subgroup showed a significant harmful association with markedly increased mortality risk (HR = 6.680, 95% CI, 2.759-16.171, p < 0.0001). These findings indicate that HTE-guided stratification can uncover clinically meaningful beneficial and harmful treatment-effect patterns that are masked in the full-cohort emulation.

</details>


### [83] [An Upper Bound on the Probability That a User Encounters an Undiscovered Defect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17061)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需分布假设的保守上界估计方法，利用beta测试中仅报告一次的缺陷比例（s/n）作为用户遇到未测试缺陷概率的估计量，为软件发布决策提供直接依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件发布决策的关键问题不是剩余缺陷数量或某个缺陷是否存在，而是用户遇到缺陷的概率。现有可靠性文献关注前者，缺乏对后者直接、分布无关的估计方法。

Method: 将每个beta测试报告视为用户群体的抽样，每个缺陷视为一个类别，证明仅报告一次的缺陷比例s/n是用户遇到未测试缺陷概率的保守上界。该估计是规范瓮构造下未见缺陷质量的极大似然估计，不依赖操作剖面、缺陷数量/频率假设或程序内部结构模型。

Result: s/n估计量自动为保守上界（高估而非低估用户风险），对缺陷的可达性图结构（树或有向无环图）不变，隐藏在其他缺陷后面的缺陷自动被上界覆盖。通过合成群体验证了估计器的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为软件发布决策提供了简单、鲁棒、无需分布假设的实用工具，适用于beta测试或崩溃遥测数据场景。

Abstract: Before releasing software to a general population, a developer must weigh a single question: if we ship now, what fraction of users will still hit a defect? This is not a question about how many defects remain, nor whether any particular defect is present -- the quantities the reliability literature has long estimated -- but about a different and, for a release decision, more consequential one: the probability that a user encounters a defect at all. We give a direct, distribution-free answer. Reading each beta-test report as a draw from the user population and each distinct defect as a class, we show that the fraction of defects reported exactly once, $s/n$, is a conservative upper bound on the probability that a user encounters a defect unseen in testing. This bound is the exact maximum-likelihood estimate of the mass of unseen defects under a general urn construction -- the canonical form -- into which any population of classes embeds; because that construction charges every singleton to the unseen reservoir, $s/n$ overstates the user's risk rather than understating it, the direction a release decision requires. The estimate needs no operational profile, no assumption on the number or frequency of defects, and no model of the program's internal structure -- since a defect's report count already reflects how many users reach it, the estimate is invariant to whether the reachability graph is a tree or a directed acyclic graph, and defects hidden behind other defects are bounded automatically. We validate the estimator against synthetic populations with known ground truth, and discuss the encounter-level data -- beta or crash telemetry -- under which the user-facing reading holds.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验分层贝叶斯(HB)可信区间在多重压力测试场景下的频率论覆盖率表现，特别是领域层面的覆盖偏差问题，并探索先验敏感性和Prasad-Rao MSE修正两种恢复策略的有效性。

Method: 扩展蒙特卡洛模拟，涵盖140个层和13个估计域，加入经典直接估计量作为基准，测试先验敏感性和PR MSE修正两种策略。在四个压力测试场景下评估HB与经典估计量在国家和领域层面的覆盖率。

Result: 国家层面HB覆盖近名义值（就业93-96%，失业87-97%，工时99.5-100%）；领域层面工时覆盖良好（94-98%），但就业和失业在低域间异质性场景下覆盖率不足；稀有事件场景中经典直接估计量因未抽样层偏差降至0%覆盖率，而HB以15%成本达96-100%覆盖。

Conclusion: 领域层面覆盖失败是由HB收缩向国家均值导致的偏差驱动，而非方差问题，因此先验弱化或PR MSE修正等方差膨胀手段无法可靠恢复覆盖率，需要直接处理偏差源。

Abstract: A companion paper to Tam (2026) reports an extended Monte Carlo (MC) study examining the frequentist coverage of 95% hierarchical Bayes (HB) credible intervals at the national and domain levels under four stress-test scenarios. The extended study covers 140 strata and 13 estimation domains separately, adds a classical direct-estimator benchmark, and tests two strategies for restoring domain coverage: prior sensitivity and Prasad and Rao (PR) MSE correction. At the national level, HB credible intervals achieve near-nominal coverage across all four scenarios and all three labour force variables (Employment 93 to 96%, Unemployment 87 to 97%, Hours Worked 99.5 to 100%). At the domain level, Hours Worked coverage is nearnominal (94 to 98%) in all scenarios; Employment and Unemployment coverage is below nominal for scenarios with low between-domain heterogeneity, a direct consequence of HB shrinkage toward the national mean. A key operational finding emerges from the Rare Event scenario (D): the classical direct estimator collapses to 0% national coverage for Employment and Hours Worked because five unsampled strata introduce a systematic bias; the HB estimator achieves 96 to 100% national coverage at roughly 15% of the classical sample cost. Neither a weaker prior nor PR MSE correction reliably restores domain coverage, confirming that the failure is bias-driven and cannot be remedied by variance inflation alone.

</details>


### [85] [PIONEER: Bayesian Joint Modelling of Mechanistic Tumour Growth and Time-to-Event Endpoints for Dynamic Prediction of Ongoing Oncology Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17908)
*Karim Naguib,Roger Berché,Lu Li,Antonia Bevan,Sajan Khosla,Jessica Davies,Paul Metcalfe*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出PIONEER贝叶斯联合建模框架，将肿瘤尺寸动态的机械性状态空间子模型与竞争性临床事件的多状态比例风险子模型耦合，实现从稀疏、不成熟的生存数据中提前预测临床终点。在小细胞肺癌案例中，该模型能在入组仅4个月（9名患者）时生成校准的PFS预测，覆盖成熟的19个月Kaplan-Meier曲线；11个月（39名患者）时OS预测收敛，提前至少8个月提供量化不确定性的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤学临床试验中，高风险的决策常需要在生存数据尚不成熟时做出（PFS和OS高度删失、事件积累少、主要终点需数月甚至数年才能读出），而 interim 数据切面已有的是信息丰富的纵向肿瘤测量和基线协变量，需利用这些信息提前进行预测以支持早期决策。

Method: PIONEER：贝叶斯联合建模框架，包含（1）机械性双组分状态空间子模型，从稀疏、有噪的SLD观测中推断潜在的患者个体肿瘤轨迹（分解为治疗响应和难治两个分区，采用Gompertz衰减生长）；（2）多状态比例风险子模型，以潜在轨迹作为时变协变量，事件数据同时通过联合似然反哺优化肿瘤动力学参数。所有临床终点（PFS、OS、ORR）均从联合后验中通过单次前向模拟推导，传播完整参数不确定性。

Result: 在广泛期小细胞肺癌案例（两个试验，N=497）中，留未来交叉验证表明：入组第4个月（9名患者）时模型生成的PFS预测已校准，覆盖成熟的19个月Kaplan-Meier曲线；入组第11个月（39名患者）时OS预测收敛，实现至少8个月的提前预测并带有恰当量化的不确定性。

Conclusion: 期望该工作推动贝叶斯机械性状态空间框架在临床开发中的更广泛应用，使基于不成熟试验数据的早期、更明智决策成为可能。

Abstract: High-stakes decisions in oncology clinical trials must often be made while survival data remains immature: progression-free survival (PFS) and overall survival (OS) are heavily censored, few events have accumulated, and the primary endpoint may be months or years from reading out. What is available at interim data cut-offs is information-rich longitudinal tumour measurements and baseline covariates. We present PIONEER, a Bayesian joint modelling framework that couples a mechanistic two-component state-space submodel of longitudinal tumour size dynamics to a multistate proportional-hazard submodel for competing clinical events, fitted simultaneously under a single posterior. The mechanistic submodel infers latent per-patient tumour trajectories - decomposed into treatment-responsive and refractory compartments with Gompertz-attenuated growth - from sparse, noisy sum-of-longest-diameter (SLD) observations. These latent trajectories feed the multistate hazard as time-varying covariates, while the event data simultaneously refines the tumour dynamics through the joint likelihood. All clinical endpoints (PFS, OS, objective response rate) are derived from the joint posterior in a single forward simulation pass, propagating full parameter uncertainty without any two-stage plug-in. Applied to a case study in extensive-stage small-cell lung cancer (two trials, N = 497), leave-future-out cross-validation demonstrates that at month 4 of enrolment (9 patients) the model produces calibrated PFS forecasts covering the mature month-19 Kaplan-Meier curve, and at month 11 (39 patients) the OS forecast converges - representing at least 8 months of advance forecasting with properly quantified uncertainty. We hope this work paves the way for broader adoption of Bayesian mechanistic state-space frameworks in clinical development, enabling earlier and more informed decision-making from immature trial data.

</details>


### [86] [Privacy-preserving causal mediation analysis using distributed electronic health record networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17958)

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出了一种隐私保护的联邦中介分析框架，使多机构能在不共享患者级数据的情况下估计治疗的直接和间接效应，并在实际EHR数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR网络为大规模研究治疗机制提供了机会，但跨机构的中介分析常因隐私和治理约束而受阻，无法共享患者级数据。

Method: 将可再生学习与反事实因果中介分析相结合，各机构仅需交换低维汇总统计量，即可联合估计自然直接效应和间接效应。

Result: 模拟研究和真实应用均表明，联邦估计器在保护患者隐私的同时，能够紧密复现 pooled 数据分析结果。在32,146名患者中，BMI介导的通路仅占GLP-1受体激动剂对HbA1c降低总效应的一小部分。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了跨机构中介分析的隐私瓶颈，并揭示GLP-1受体激动剂的降糖效果主要通过体重减轻以外的机制实现。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) networks provide unprecedented opportunities to study treatment mechanisms at scale, but mediation analyses across institutions are often hindered by privacy and governance constraints that restrict sharing of patient-level data. We developed a privacy-preserving federated mediation framework that enables estimation of natural direct and indirect effects without exchanging individual-level records across participating sites. The proposed approach integrates renewable learning with counterfactual causal mediation analysis, allowing institutions to collaboratively investigate treatment mechanisms using only low-dimensional summary statistics. Both simulation studies and the real-world application demonstrated that the federated estimator closely reproduced pooled-data results while preserving patient privacy. We applied the method to 32,146 patients in the Indiana Network for Patient Care to evaluate the extent to which body mass index (BMI) mediates the effect of GLP-1 receptor agonist on glycated hemoglobin (HbA1c) reduction. The BMI-mediated pathway accounted for only a small proportion of the overall treatment effect, suggesting that most glycemic improvement occurred through mechanisms other than weight loss.

</details>


### [87] [Predicting subjective rage and facial expressions in human driving: A Bayesian network approach with beta-distributed nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18030)
*Zaïra Méndez-Porcar,Francisco Palmí-Perales,Gabriel Calvo,Carmen Armero,Ana de la Torre-García*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于贝叶斯网络的框架，用于对单位区间连续变量进行建模，使用条件Beta分布节点在完全贝叶斯推断环境下工作，并通过MCMC方法（WinBUGS实现）进行推断。应用于情绪与面部反应实验，发现眉毛降低与愤怒强度强相关且男性更频繁，上眼睑抬起与刺激相关但不依赖愤怒或性别，模型可从面部手势预测愤怒严重程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在建模单位区间连续变量（如概率、比例数据）时，难以同时捕获条件依赖关系并有效传播不确定性。因此需要一种能够在贝叶斯框架下处理此类变量、支持条件依赖建模且实现完整不确定性量化的方法。

Method: 构建贝叶斯网络，其中每个节点采用条件Beta分布来建模单位区间变量；使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法在WinBUGS软件中进行完全贝叶斯推断；网络结构允许捕获变量间的条件依赖关系并传播不确定性。

Result: 在情绪与面部反应实验中：眉毛降低与愤怒强度显著相关，且在男性中更频繁；上眼睑抬起在刺激条件下增加，但与愤怒强度或性别无关；模型能够根据信息性面部手势预测愤怒严重程度。

Abstract: A Bayesian network framework is proposed for modelling unit-bounded continuous variables using conditional beta-distributed nodes within a fully Bayesian inference setting. The model captures conditional dependencies and propagates uncertainty through the network, with inference performed via Markov Chain Monte Carlo methods implemented in WinBUGS. The framework is applied to an experimental study of emotional and facial responses, focusing on rage intensity and facial gestures. Results show that brow lowering is strongly associated with rage intensity and is more frequent in men, whereas upper lid raising decreases under provocation independently of rage or sex. The model also predicts rage severity from informative facial gestures.

</details>


### [88] [A binomial-like probability distribution with heavy tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18083)
*Jürgen Groß*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种二元分布的简单替代方案，通过加权算术平均合并二项分布与新引入的双均匀分布，以在尾部赋予更多概率权重。文中推导了相关性质，并应用于经典数据集，展示了从模型构建、性质推导、参数估计到假设检验的完整统计建模流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二项分布在尾部概率权重不足，难以有效拟合某些实际数据中的极端值。作者旨在提出一种更灵活、尾部概率更重的替代分布，以改善对实际数据的拟合效果。

Method: 将二项分布与新提出的双均匀分布（bi-uniform distribution）进行加权算术平均，得到新的混合分布。随后推导其统计性质，并采用估计方法对实际数据进行拟合，最后通过假设检验验证模型适用性。

Result: 得到了新分布的概率质量函数及其相关统计性质；在经典数据集上的应用表明该分布能够更好地捕获尾部概率，拟合效果优于标准二项分布。

Conclusion: 通过加权平均构造尾部加重分布是一种简单有效的方法；该研究同时作为统计建模完整流程的示范案例，涵盖了模型构建、性质分析、参数估计与假设检验等关键环节。

Abstract: A simple alternative to the binomial distribution that places more probability weight on the tails is considered. Its derivation only requires the weighted arithmetic mean of two discrete probability mass functions, one being the binomial itself and the other being the bi-uniform introduced here. Some properties are derived, and an application to a classical data set is discussed. The presentation can be seen as an exemplary treatise on how to construct a statistical model, derive statistical properties, fit models to actual data by employing estimation methods, and verify appropriateness by using elements from statistical hypothesis testing.

</details>

### [89] [Exceptional Sets for Certain ${}_2F_1$ Hypergeometric Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16331)
*Archisman Bhattacharjee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过超几何-模恒等式、Wüstholz的周期超越性结果以及Schneider关于CM椭圆曲线j-不变量的经典结果，显式确定了单值群为Takeuchi I类算术三角形群的高斯超几何函数的有理参数例外集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整分类并显式刻画有理参数(a,b,c)下高斯超几何函数的例外集E(a,b,c)，特别针对单值群为Takeuchi I类算术三角形群的情形。

Method: 结合超几何-模恒等式将问题转化为模函数的代数点问题；利用Wüstholz关于阿贝尔簇周期的超越性结果；运用Schneider关于CM椭圆曲线j-不变量代数值的经典定理。

Result: 完整确定了超几何函数单值群属于Takeuchi I类算术三角形群时对应的例外集E(a,b,c)。

Conclusion: 为有理参数超几何函数例外集的完整分类提供了关键一步，展示了超越数论与经典特殊函数理论的深度结合。

Abstract: For $a,b,c \in \mathbb{Q}$, the exceptional set associated to the Gauss hypergeometric function $_2F_1(a,b,c;z)$ is defined by $E(a,b,c) := \{ z \in \overline{\Q} \mid {}_2F_1(a,b,c;z) \in \overline{\mathbb{Q}} \}.$ In this paper, the exceptional sets $E(a,b,c)$ are determined explicitly for each $_2F_1(a,b,c;z)$ whose monodromy group is an arithmetic triangle group in Takeuchi's class I. The description is obtained via hypergeometric--modular identities together with transcendence results for periods of abelian varieties due to Wüstholz, and classical result of Schneider on algebraic values of $j$-invariant of elliptic curves with complex multiplication.

</details>


### [90] [A Complete classification of the rank of a certain family of elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16335)
*Richa Sharma,Arkabrata Ghosh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了椭圆曲线族 $E_{p}: y^{2}=x^{3}-6px$（$p\neq 3$ 为奇素数）的秩，给出了基于素数 $p$ 的同余类完全分类：当 $p\equiv 1 \pmod{24}$ 时秩为1或3，其他情形（$p\neq 3$）秩为1，且 $E_3$ 秩为0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对特定形式的椭圆曲线族 $E_p: y^2 = x^3 - 6px$ 的秩进行完整分类，填补对该族曲线秩结构认识的空白。

Method: 通过分析素数 $p$ 的同余类（模24），利用数论和椭圆曲线理论证明不同同余类下秩的取值。

Result: 1) 当 $p\equiv 1 \pmod{24}$ 时，$E_p$ 的秩为1或3；2) 当 $p$ 为其他奇素数（$p\neq 3$）时，秩恰好为1；3) $E_3: y^2=x^3-18x$ 的秩为0。

Conclusion: 给出了该椭圆曲线族秩关于素数参数 $p$ 同余类的完整分类。

Abstract: In this article, we study the rank of the family of elliptic curves $E_{p}: y^{2}=x^{3}-6px, $ where $p\neq 3$ is an odd prime. We prove that if $p\equiv 1 \pmod{24}$, then the rank of $E_{p}$ is either $1$ or $3$, whereas for all other congruence classes of the odd prime $p$, the rank of $E_{p}$ is exactly $1$. Furthermore, we show that the elliptic curve $ E_{3}: y^{2}=x^{3}-18x $ has rank $0$. Thus, our results provide a complete classification of the ranks of the curves in this family according to the residue class of the prime parameter $p$.

</details>

*Vladimir Nechaev*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了权为k、关于同余子群Γ₀(2)的弱全纯模形式傅里叶系数的算术性质，此类形式仅在尖点∞处有极点。利用典范基结构及仅在∞处有极点的弱全纯模形式空间系数之间的显式对偶性，证明了模素数p≥5的一般同余族，并推导出若干推论作为定理应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弱全纯模形式傅里叶系数的算术性质，特别是模素数同余关系，以深化对模形式算术理论的理解。

Method: 利用典范基结构以及仅在尖点∞处有极点的弱全纯模形式空间系数之间的显式对偶性，进行同余关系的证明。

Result: 证明了关于模素数p≥5的一般同余族，并推导出多个具体推论。

Conclusion: 所建立的一般同余族及其推论展示了该方法在弱全纯模形式算术性质研究中的有效性，并为后续相关研究提供了理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the arithmetic properties of the Fourier coefficients of weakly holomorphic modular forms of weight $k$ for the congruence subgroup $Γ_0(2)$, which have poles exclusively at the cusp $\infty$. Using the structure of canonical bases and the explicit duality between the coefficients of spaces of weakly holomorphic modular forms, which have a pole only at $\infty$, we prove a general family of congruences modulo primes $p \ge 5$ and derive several corollaries as examples of the theorem's application.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动力学系统中有理环的分布问题，特别是在p-adic数域上的算术动力学性质。

Result: 得到了参数c∈ℤ_p使得x^d+c存在ℚ_p有理n-环的密度渐近公式；当d=2且p≠2,3时，给出了n=1,2,3的显式密度值。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了在p-adic数域上，多项式动力学中周期轨道存在的参数密度具有可计算的渐近行为。

Abstract: We prove an asymptotic formula for the density of parameters $c\in \mathbb{Z}_p$ for which the polynomial $x^d+c$ admits a $\mathbb{Q}_p$-rational $n$-cycle. In the cases where $d=2$ and $p\neq 2,3$, we compute these densities explicitly for $n=1,2,$ and $3$.

</details>

*H. S. Sumanth Bharadwaj,N. Sujatha,S. Chandankumar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推导了函数 $\overline{p}_{\ell}(n)$ 的拉马努金型同余式族，该函数计算 $n$ 的 $2$-色超划分，其中一种颜色仅出现在 $\ell$ 倍数部分。例如，对所有 $n\geq0$，证明 $\overline{p}_{4}(32n+28)\equiv 0 \pmod{512}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究特殊超划分函数 $\overline{p}_{\ell}(n)$ 的算术性质，探索其模幂同余模式，扩展拉马努金型同余理论。

Method: 通过解析推导或模形式理论，建立 $\overline{p}_{\ell}(n)$ 的一般同余族，并给出具体数值例证。

Result: 获得了 $\overline{p}_{\ell}(n)$ 的拉马努金型同余族，如 $\overline{p}_{4}(32n+28)\equiv 0 \pmod{512}$。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 $2$-色超划分在限制颜色出现条件下的深层模同余结构，丰富了划分同余理论。

Abstract: We derive general families of Ramanujan-type congruences for the function $\overline{p}_{\ell}(n)$, which counts the $2$-color overpartitions of $n$ in which one of the colors appears only in parts that are multiples of $\ell$. For example, for all $n\geq0$ we prove that $$\overline{p}_{4}(32n+28)\equiv 0 \pmod{512}.$$

</details>


### [94] [Solutions to Two Problems of Sárközy and Sós on Additive Representation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16613)
*Peiru Kuang,Yan Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Sárközy和Sós在1997年提出的两个关于加法表示函数的问题：1) 若无限集A满足对充分大的m有r₂(A,2m+1)≥r₂(A,2m)，则A的补集有限（否定回答Problem 3.1）；2) 存在一个趋于无穷、单调不减且增长阶为o(n/(log n)²)的算术函数f，以及一个集合A，使得|r₁(A,n)−f(n)|=o((f(n))^{1/2})在密度为1的整数序列上成立（肯定回答Problem 3.3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Erdős、Sárközy和Sós提出的加法表示函数理论中两个长期未决的问题，特别是Sárközy和Sós在1997年明确提出的问题3.1和3.3，以深化对加法基结构及其表示函数性质的理解。

Method: 采用组合数论和解析数论方法，通过构造反例和正例来分别回答两个问题。对于问题3.1，通过分析r₂函数的奇偶性差异与集合补集有限性之间的关系，构造矛盾证明；对于问题3.3，构造满足特定增长条件的目标函数f及相应集合A，利用概率方法或显式构造来逼近该函数。

Result: 1) 证明了若无限集A满足r₂(A,2m+1)≥r₂(A,2m)对所有充分大的m成立，则A的补集是有限的；2) 构造了满足给定条件的函数f和集合A，使得|r₁(A,n)−f(n)|的误差相对于f(n)^{1/2}可忽略不计，且该逼近在密度为1的整数上成立。

Conclusion: 分别对Sárközy和Sós的两个问题给出了否定与肯定的答案，澄清了加法表示函数理论中的关键猜想，揭示了集合结构与表示函数渐近行为之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: For a set $A\subseteq\mathbb{N}_0$, let $r_1(A,n)$ denote the number of solutions of the equation $a+a^{\prime}=n$ with $a,a^{\prime}\in A$, and let $r_2(A,n)$ denote the number of such solutions subject to $a\le a^{\prime}$. These functions are called additive representation functions (as first considered by Erdős, Sárközy and Sós). In this paper, we resolve two problems posed by Sárközy and Sós in 1997.
  First, if $A$ is infinite and $r_2(A,2m+1)\ge r_2(A,2m)$ for every sufficiently large $m$, then the complement of $A$ is finite. This gives a negative answer to Problem 3.1 in~\cite{SarkozySos1997}.
  Secondly, there exist an arithmetic function $f$ satisfying $f(n) \to \infty$, $f(n+1) \ge f(n)$ for $n > n_0$, and $f(n) = o\left(\frac{n}{(\log n)^2}\right)$, and a set $A$ such that \( |r_1(A,n) - f(n)| = o((f(n))^{1/2}) \) holds on a sequence of integers $n$ whose density is $1$. This gives a positive answer to Problem 3.3 in~\cite{SarkozySos1997}.

</details>


### [95] [Counterexamples of Friedlander--Iwaniec dual sums conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16695)
*Khai-Hoan Nguyen-Dang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对Friedlander和Iwaniec关于截断非线性对偶和的一个猜想给出了反例。具体而言，对于$A(s)=B(s)=\zeta(s)^m$且$m\geq4$的情形，该猜想中的上界估计$\mathcal B_{\ell,D}(x,N) \ll_{\varepsilon,\boldsymbol\kappa} (DNx)^\varepsilon$不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Friedlander和Iwaniec（2005）定义了 sharply truncated nonlinear dual sum $\mathcal B_{\ell,D}(x,N)$，并提出了一个关于其上界的猜想（Conjecture 1），即该和可以被$(DNx)^\varepsilon$一致地控制。该猜想若成立，将对解析数论中相关问题的研究具有重要价值。

Method: 本文选取$A(s)=B(s)=\zeta(s)^m$（$m\geq4$）这一特殊情形，其中$\zeta(s)$为Riemann zeta函数，通过构造具体的反例来检验Friedlander-Iwaniec猜想的有效性。

Result: 发现当$m\geq4$时，Friedlander和Iwaniec的猜想预测不再成立，即$\mathcal B_{\ell,D}(x,N)$的上界不能由$(DNx)^\varepsilon$一致控制。

Conclusion: Friedlander和Iwaniec关于截断非线性对偶和的上界猜想存在反例，因此该猜想在一般情况下不成立，至少需要对条件做进一步的修正或限制。

Abstract: Let $a(n)$ and $b(n)$ be arithmetic sequences, and $$A(s)=\sum_{n\ge1}a(n)n^{-s}, \qquad B(s)=\sum_{n\ge1}b(n)n^{-s},$$ be the two Dirichlet series related by a certain functional equation. Let $m$ be the \emph{analytic degree} of the functional equation. For $x>0$ and a positive integer $N$, Friedlander and Iwaniec (2005) define the sharply truncated nonlinear dual sum $$\mathcal B_{\ell,D}(x,N) := \sum_{\substack{n\in\mathbb N\\ n\le N}} b(n)n^{-β_m} \cos\left( 2πm\left(\frac{nx}{D}\right)^{1/m} +\frac{π\ell}{4} \right),$$ where $D\ge1$ is the conductor, $β_m:=\frac{m+1}{2m}$, and $\ell=m-3-2k$ is determined by the archimedean weight $k$ of the functional equation. Their Conjecture 1 predicts that, for every $\varepsilon>0$, $$\mathcal B_{\ell,D}(x,N) \ll_{\varepsilon,\boldsymbolκ} (DNx)^\varepsilon,$$ uniformly in the variables $x$ and $N$, with the degree, conductor, and archimedean datum fixed. We give counterexamples to this prediction with $$A(s)=B(s)=ζ(s)^m,\; m\geq 4$$ where $$ζ(s):=\sum_{n\ge1}n^{-s} \qquad(\operatorname{Re}s>1)$$ is the Riemann zeta function.

</details>


### [96] [An explicit uniform cubic wedge for consecutive Toeplitz minors of the Riemann xi coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16795)
*Wojciech Michalowski*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了归一化Riemann xi-函数的正系数序列(a_k)的Toeplitz余子式D_{r,k}在r≥2且k≥10^18 r^3时严格大于0，给出了显式的三次尾标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riemann假设等价于(a_k)是无限阶Polya频率序列，进而等价于所有Toeplitz余子式非负。本文旨在对尾区域（k远大于r^3）建立显式、一致于r的正性结论。

Method: 结合认证的复鞍点分析矩变换、精确的q-Pascal膨胀半群同时控制所有度数、加权Banach代数控制非线性余项，最后通过惯性保持论证封闭。所有解析常数使用Arb球算法的有向舍入认证，代数恒等式在精确有理算术中验证。

Result: 对任意r≥2和k≥10^18 r^3，D_{r,k} > 0。这是一个与Katkova固定阶渐近正性不同的、显式三次尾标度一致结果。

Conclusion: 该结果仅涉及尾区域（k远大于r^3），对于互补区域（即Riemann假设所关注的区域）未取得进展。

Abstract: Let (a_k) be the positive coefficient sequence of the normalized Riemann xi-function, and let D_{r,k} denote its consecutive Toeplitz minors. The Riemann Hypothesis is equivalent to (a_k) being a Polya frequency sequence of infinite order, and hence to nonnegativity of all Toeplitz minors. We prove that D_{r,k} > 0 for every r >= 2 and k >= 10^18 r^3. This gives an explicit cubic tail scale uniform in r, in contrast with Katkova's fixed-order asymptotic positivity. The proof does not use numerically verified zeros of the Riemann zeta-function. It combines a certified complex saddle-point analysis of the moment transform, an exact q-Pascal dilation semigroup controlling every degree simultaneously, and a weighted Banach-algebra majorant for the nonlinear remainder, closed by an inertia-preservation argument. All analytic constants are certified with directed rounding in Arb ball arithmetic, and all algebraic identities are verified in exact rational arithmetic. The ancillary files reproduce every certificate. The result concerns only the tail regime k much larger than r^3 and makes no progress on the Riemann Hypothesis, which concerns the complementary region.

</details>


### [97] [Twisted Siegel-Weil formulas for $\mathrm{GL}_2$ over non-Galois quartic CM fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16893)
*Mingkuan Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了非伽罗瓦四次CM域的扭曲Siegel-Weil公式，将扭曲theta积分与Doi-Naganuma提升的Hecke积分等同起来，并应用于证明Borcherds形式的扭曲CM值是代数单位对数的倍数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立非伽罗瓦四次CM域的扭曲Siegel-Weil公式，将扭曲theta积分与Doi-Naganuma提升的Hecke积分等同起来，从而建立某些Hecke特征从Q到实二次域的Jacquet-Langlands对应的基变换是同构的。

Method: 使用扭曲Siegel-Weil公式，结合Doi-Naganuma提升和Hecke积分，以及Jacquet-Langlands对应的基变换。

Result: 建立了非伽罗瓦四次CM域的扭曲Siegel-Weil公式；证明了某些Hecke特征的Jacquet-Langlands对应基变换是从Q到实二次域的同构。

Conclusion: Borcherds形式的扭曲CM值是代数单位对数的倍数，可由扭曲theta积分的Fourier系数显式描述。

Abstract: We establish twisted Siegel-Weil formulas for non-Galois quartic CM fields, identifying the twisted theta integral against a quadratic character with the Doi-Naganuma lift of Hecke's integral. This implies the base change of Jacquet-Langlands correspondence for certain Hecke characters from $\mathbb{Q}$ to real quadratic fields via Doi-Naganuma lift is an isomorphism. As an application, we prove that the twisted CM values of Borcherds forms are algebraic multiple of logarithm of units, explicitly described by the Fourier coefficients of twisted theta integrals.

</details>


### [98] [A Modular Form Proof of the Irrationality of $ζ\left(3\right)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17123)
*Pang Ern Thang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过构造级数为6的模形式所对应的Eichler积分，得到了具有可控分母和足够大收敛半径的幂级数，然后利用Beukers无理数判定准则，证明了ζ(3)的无理性。文章以阐述性证明的形式呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apéry常数ζ(3)的无理性是数论中的一个经典结果，本文旨在提供一个基于模形式的新颖且阐述性的证明途径，使该结果更易于理解。

Method: 利用级数为6的模形式构造合适的Eichler积分，由此生成一个具有可控分母和足够大收敛半径的幂级数，再应用Beukers无理数判定准则。

Result: 成功证明了ζ(3)是无理数。

Conclusion: 模形式方法为证明ζ(3)的无理性提供了一种简洁而优雅的途径，展示了模形式与解析数论之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We present an expository proof of the irrationality of $ζ\left(3\right)$ using modular forms of level 6. By constructing a suitable Eichler integral, we obtain a power series with controlled denominators and sufficiently large radius of convergence. Beukers' irrationality criterion then implies that $ζ\left(3\right)$ is irrational.

</details>


### [99] [On the average number of subgroups of the group ${\Bbb Z}_{n_1}\times {\Bbb Z}_{n_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17245)
*Zhai Wenguang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限交换群 Z_{n1}×Z_{n2} 中子群个数 s(n1,n2) 和循环子群个数 c(n1,n2) 的二元求和函数的渐近行为，给出了更精确的渐近结果并研究了误差项的均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入理解有限交换群中子群与循环子群计数的二元均值分布规律，改进已有渐近结果。

Method: 利用数论方法（如Dirichlet卷积、生成函数、渐近展开等）对 s(n1,n2) 和 c(n1,n2) 在 n1,n2≤x 上的二元和进行渐近分析，并研究误差项的均值性质。

Result: 给出了 ∑_{n1,n2≤x} s(n1,n2) 和 ∑_{n1,n2≤x} c(n1,n2) 的更精确渐近公式，以及相应误差项的均值结果。

Conclusion: 改进了关于有限交换群子群与循环子群二元计数求和函数的渐近结果，并揭示了误差项的均值行为。

Abstract: Let ${\mathbb Z}_{n}$ be the additive group of residue classes modulo $n.$ For any positive integers $n_1$ and $n_2$, let $s(n_1,n_2)$ and $c(n_1,n_2)$ denote the number of subgroups and the number of cyclic subgroups of the group ${\mathbb Z}_{n_1}\times {\mathbb Z}_{n_2}$, respectively. The aim of this paper is to study the asymptotic behavior of the sums $\sum_{n_1,n_2\le x}s(n_1,n_2)$ and $\sum_{n_1,n_2\le x}c(n_1,n_2)$. Some sharper asymptotic results are given for the these sums. Mean values of the error terms are also studied.

</details>


### [100] [A survey on moments of a class of error terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17249)
*Zhai Wenguang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文综述了解析数论中一类误差项的矩，回顾了Dirichlet除数问题矩，以及除数问题中误差项的混合矩，给出了Δ₂(x)和Δ₃(x)符号变化联合分布的两个新结果，并提出了Selberg类中矩结果的若干猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理解析数论中除数问题误差项的矩理论，填补符号变化联合分布研究的空白，并推动Selberg类框架下矩问题的理论发展。

Method: 文献综述结合新结果推导：回顾已有矩结果，分析Δ₂(x)和Δ₃(x)符号变化的联合分布，并基于Selberg类公理提出猜想。

Result: 给出了Δ₂(x)和Δ₃(x)符号变化联合分布的两个新定理，提出了Selberg类中若干矩猜想。

Conclusion: 该综述统一了除数问题误差项矩理论的现有成果，通过新结果推进了符号变化联合分布的理解，并为Selberg类中的矩研究指明了方向。

Abstract: This is a survey article on the moments of a class of error terms in analytic number theory. We begin by reviewing the moments of the Dirichlet divisor problem, and then review the hybrid moments of error terms in the divisor problems. We give two new results on the joint distribution of sign changes of $Δ_2(x)$ and $Δ_3(x). $ In the last section, we present some conjectures on the moment results in the well-known Selberg class.

</details>


### [101] [Consecutive non-square non-primitive tuples in finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17267)
*Juncheng Zhou,Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有限域中连续非平方非本原（NSNP）元素的存在性问题，给出了连续非平方ℓ次幂元的存在性定理，并证明了当θ_q<4/15时存在三个连续NSNP元素，其中常数4/15是最优的，仅三个例外域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域中连续非平方非本原元素的存在性条件，并确定最优边界常数。

Method: 先建立连续非平方ℓ次幂元的一般存在性定理（利用字符和估计与Weil界），再结合该定理与有限域上的数值计算，推得关于三个连续NSNP元素存在性的阈值结果。

Result: 当q>(k-1)²(2ℓ)^{2k}时存在k个连续非平方ℓ次幂元；θ_q<4/15保证存在三个连续NSNP元素；θ_q=4/15时仅三个例外域F_31、F_61、F_121；4/15为最优常数。

Conclusion: 给出了连续NSNP元素存在性的精确阈值，证明了4/15是可达且最优的边界。

Abstract: Let $q$ be an odd prime power and put \[
  θ_q=\frac{\varphi(q-1)}{q-1}. \] Let $\mathbb{F}_q$ denote a finite field with $q$ elements, an element of $\mathbb{F}_q$ is called non-square non-primitive, or \emph{NSNP}, if it is both a non-square and a non-primitive element. We first obtain a general existence theorem for consecutive tuples of non-square $\ell$th powers, where $\ell$ is an odd prime divisor of $q-1$. More precisely, if $k\ge 2$, $\text{char}\mathbb{F}_q\ge k$, and \[
  q>(k-1)^2(2\ell)^{2k}, \] then $\mathbb{F}_q$ contains $k$ consecutive non-square $\ell$th powers. Combining this result with a finite computation, we prove that $θ_q<4/15$ guarantees the existence of three consecutive NSNP elements. On the boundary $θ_q=4/15$, the only exceptions are \[ \mathbb{F}_{31},\quad \mathbb{F}_{61},\quad \mathbb{F}_{121}. \] In particular, the constant $4/15$ is best possible.

</details>


### [102] [Torsion of rational elliptic curves over $\Z_p$-extensions of quadratic fields for $p\geq5$, with a slope analysis for $p=3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17303)
*Shisong Xu,Haoyang Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了椭圆曲线在二次域$\Z_p$-扩张上的挠点稳定性问题，修正了Avcı定理的证明缺陷，将$p>5$的结果推广到$p\geq5$，并为$p=3$且$K\neq\Q(\sqrt{-3})$的情形给出了斜率分层理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Avcı定理原证明存在一个伽罗瓦性缺陷：有限层在一般混合$\Z_p$-扩张中未必是$\Q$上的伽罗瓦扩张，而辅助下降引理需要$\Q$上伽罗瓦条件。本文旨在修复该缺陷并推广结果。

Method: 通过用分圆和反分圆方向构造有限伽罗瓦包络替换原有的有限层，修复了伽罗瓦性缺陷。利用二次扭和Chou–Daniels–Krijan–Najman在$\Q_{\infty,5}$上的定理排除$25$阶循环挠点障碍。对$p=3$情形引入斜率分层方法，分析各斜率剩余类上的挠点类型。

Result: 将Avcı定理从$p>5$推广到$p\geq5$（包括$p=5$）；对$p=3$且$K\neq\Q(\sqrt{-3})$给出了挠点结构的完整刻画：奇挠点来自分圆层，$2$-primary挠点来自$E[2]$层；反分圆类仅贡献$2$-primary增长；新奇挠点仅出现在分圆剩余类中。奇循环子群的阶有明确上界，$13$-挠点在分层覆盖情形中被排除。

Conclusion: 完整解决了二次域$\Z_p$-扩张上椭圆曲线挠点稳定性的若干情形，修正了原定理的证明缺陷并获得了更优的结果。

Abstract: Let $E/\Q$ be an elliptic curve, let $K$ be a quadratic field, and let $L/K$ be a $\Z_p$-extension. We revisit Avcı's theorem on the equality $E(L)_{\tors}=E(K)_{\tors}$ for $p>5$ and clarify a Galois-over-$\Q$ point needed in its proof: the auxiliary descent lemmas are finite Galois-over-$\Q$ statements, while finite layers of a general mixed $\Z_p$-extension of an imaginary quadratic field need not be Galois over $\Q$. We repair this by replacing finite layers by finite Galois envelopes built from the cyclotomic and anti-cyclotomic directions. We then prove the equality $E(L)_{\tors}=E(K)_{\tors}$ for every $p\geq5$, including the formerly exceptional prime $p=5$, by excluding the remaining cyclic $25$-torsion obstruction via quadratic twists and the theorem of Chou--Daniels--Krijan--Najman over $\Q_{\infty,5}$. Finally, for $p=3$ and imaginary quadratic $K\neq\Q(\sqrt{-3})$, we give a slope stratification: the odd part of $E(L)_{\tors}$ comes from $L\cap K_{\cyc}$, while the $2$-primary part comes from $L\cap K(E[2])$. In particular, the two residue classes with both slope coordinates $3$-adic units have no torsion growth, the anti-cyclotomic residue class can contribute only $2$-primary growth, and all new odd torsion is forced into the cyclotomic residue class. The possible orders of odd cyclic subgroups are explicitly bounded, and $13$-torsion is excluded in the cases covered by the stratification.

</details>


### [103] [On Erdos-Falconer distance problem in even dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17324)
*Thang Pham,Chun-Yen Shen,Boqing Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明偶数维Erdős-Falconer距离猜想可约化至平面情形，并得到素数域上钉距问题阈值d/2+1/4及任意有限域上三角形分布阈值(d+1)/2+1/10的新纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究偶数维有限域上Erdős-Falconer距离猜想的约化问题，将高维情形归约到平面情形，从而简化证明并改进相关问题的阈值。

Method: 通过提取定理（extraction theorem），将偶数维Erdős-Falconer距离猜想约化至二维情形，在此基础上分析钉距问题与三角形分布。

Result: 在素数域上钉距问题阈值达到d/2+1/4，在任意有限域上三角形分布阈值达到(d+1)/2+1/10，均为新纪录。

Conclusion: 偶数维Erdős-Falconer距离猜想可归结为平面情形，且所得的阈值改进了已有结果。

Abstract: Let $q$ be an odd prime power and $\mathbb{F}_q$ be the finite field of order $q$. We prove an extraction theorem for the Erdős-Falconer distance conjecture in even dimensions, showing that the conjecture for all even dimensions reduces to the planar case. As consequences, we obtain improved thresholds on the pinned distance problem and the distribution of triangles, achieving new records of $\frac{d}{2}+\frac{1}{4}$ over prime fields and $\frac{d+1}{2}+\frac{1}{10}$ over arbitrary finite fields, respectively.

</details>


### [104] [Galois-invariant Néron--Severi ranks of Fermat surfaces over number fields: a Galois module, closed forms, a threshold, and exact tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17387)
*Rifat Jumagulov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文计算了Fermat曲面在任意子域上的Galois不变Neron-Severi秩，给出了封闭形式表达式（gcd(d,6)=1情形）及d≤30的表格，并证明了有理数域上平均阶为(3/5)x²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Fermat曲面在任意数域K上的Galois不变Neron-Severi秩提供系统的计算方法，填补了已有研究中"秩层"的空白。

Method: 利用特征标平均公式ρ_K = (1/|H|)∑_{t∈H} χ_NS(t)计算秩；通过Shioda本征线分解Neron-Severi群为Qh⊕M；使用Hasse-Davenport恒等式和稳定子下降处理偶次情形；借助Gvirtz-Chen-Skorobogatov的域定义计算进行验证。

Result: 给出了gcd(d,6)=1时ρ_Q(X_d)=1+3(Ψ_2(d)-3τ(d)+2)的封闭公式；建立了域定义阈值；列出了d=4到30的精确表格（含d=14,24,28,30的例外项）；证明了平均阶∑_{d≤x,gcd(d,6)=1} ρ_Q(X_d) ~ (3/5)x²。

Conclusion: 所有结果无条件成立（基于Lefschetz定理），并提供了完整的计算验证附件（15个脚本、1270行轨道证书、完整特征标表）。

Abstract: For a Fermat surface X_d: x_0^d+x_1^d+x_2^d+x_3^d=0 we compute the Galois-invariant Neron-Severi (Picard) rank rho_K(X_d) = dim (NS(X) \otimes Q)^{Gal(Qbar/K)}: for every subfield K of Q(zeta_d) by an explicit character average -- in closed form for gcd(d,6)=1, with the rational ranks tabulated for 4 <= d <= 30 -- and for an arbitrary number field K through the reduction rho_K = rho_{K \cap L} to the field of definition L. The geometric Picard number is classical (Shioda, Aoki), and L is due to Gvirtz-Chen and Skorobogatov; here we supply the rank layer. As a Gal(Q(zeta_d)/Q)-module the zeta_d-rational Neron-Severi group is Qh \oplus M with M \otimes Q(zeta_d) monomial on the Shioda eigenlines; writing chi_NS = 1 + chi_M, we get rho_K = (1/|H|) sum_{t in H} chi_NS(t). For degree coprime to 6 we prove rho_Q(X_d) = 1 + 3(Psi_2(d) - 3 tau(d) + 2) with Psi_2 multiplicative; we establish a field-of-definition threshold with explicit Hasse-Davenport witnesses, an assembled orbit rule for even d, and the exact table for 4 <= d <= 30, where the exceptional entries at d = 14, 24, 28, 30 are proved internally by Hasse-Davenport identities and a stabilizer descent, and corroborated by exact Z[zeta_m] evaluation and by the per-character field-of-definition computation of Gvirtz-Chen--Skorobogatov. The average order is sum_{d <= x, gcd(d,6)=1} rho_Q(X_d) ~ (3/5) x^2. All results are unconditional: on a surface the algebraic classes are the rational (1,1)-classes by Lefschetz. Ancillary files provide 15 standalone exact-arithmetic scripts, a 1270-row orbit-by-orbit certificate of the exceptional layer, and the full character tables, with pinned dependencies and chained checksums.

</details>


### [105] [Quadratic residue patterns of length 4 and 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17418)
*Abdulkadyr Buchaev,Michael Tsfasman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Kiritchenko等人关于二次剩余模式的结果推广到字母表{R,N}上长度为4和5的任意单词。首先给出了素数p模二次剩余序列中给定模式S出现次数的显式公式，用低亏格超椭圆曲线上的Frobenius迹表示。其次，利用广义Sato-Tate方法描述了l=4时归一化误差项的极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Kiritchenko, Tsfasman, Vlăduţ和Zakharevich论文第一部分中关于二次剩余模式的结果，从特定情况扩展到字母表{R,N}上长度为4和5的任意单词。

Method: 1) 推导模式出现次数的显式公式，用低亏格超椭圆曲线上的Frobenius迹表示；2) 应用广义Sato-Tate方法分析l=4时归一化误差项的极限分布。

Result: 得到了长度为4和5的任意二次剩余模式计数公式，以及l=4时误差项的极限分布描述。

Conclusion: 成功将二次剩余模式分析推广到更长单词情况，并通过Sato-Tate方法给出了误差项的渐近行为。

Abstract: We generalize the results of the first part of the paper by Kiritchenko, Tsfasman, Vlăduţ and Zakharevich on quadratic residue patterns to the case of arbitrary words over the alphabet $\{R, N\}$ of length $l = 4$ and $l = 5$. First, we obtain explicit formulas for the number of occurrences of a given pattern $S$ in the sequence of quadratic residues and nonresidues modulo a prime $p$. These formulas are expressed in terms of Frobenius traces on a finite collection of low-genus hyperelliptic curves. Second, using a generalized Sato-Tate approach, we describe the limiting distributions of the normalized error term for $l = 4$.

</details>


### [106] [Diophantine analysis and the Braid group ${\bf B}_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17426)
*Wei He,Wenhao Lu,Hang Yang,Rongwei Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了三股辫子群B₃的约化Burau表示对应的特征曲面S: (z₀+z₁+z₂)²+z₀z₁=0上素数三元组的分布，发现该曲面上的素数三元组出现频率高于周围格点空间，揭示了群表示论与解析数论之间意想不到的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限生成群的有限维表示出发，特征多项式Q_π(z)蕴含了群和表示的丰富结构信息。本文是探索特征零点代数簇（称为特征曲面）数论性质这一长期项目的一部分，旨在揭示群表示论与数论之间的深层联系。

Method: 以三股辫子群B₃及其约化Burau表示为具体对象，研究特征曲面S上素数三元组的分布规律，通过与周围格点空间的统计对比来揭示其数论特性。

Result: 证明了在特征曲面S上，素数三元组出现的频率显著高于在周围格点空间中的频率。

Conclusion: 这一发现建立了群表示理论与解析数论之间意想不到的联系，为从数论角度研究群表示的结构信息提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Given a finite dimensional representation $π$ of a finitely generated group $G=\langle g_1, \ldots, g_n\rangle$, the associated characteristic polynomial is defined as $Q_π(z):=\det(z_0I+z_1π(g_1)+\cdots +z_nπ(g_n))$, and it is known to contain a good amount of structural information about $G$ and $π$. This paper is a part of an ongoing project to investigate the number-theoretic properties of the algebraic varieties (called {\em eigensurfaces}) $\{z\in \mathbb{C}^{n+1}: Q_π(z)=0\}$. Its focus is the distribution of prime triples in the eigensurface $S:=\{z\in \mathbb{C}^3: (z_0+z_1+z_2)^2+z_0z_1=0\}$ associated with the braid group ${\bf B}_3$ and its reduced Burau representation. We prove that such triples occur with higher frequency on $S$ than in the ambient lattice, revealing an unexpected connection between group representation theory and analytic number theory.

</details>


### [107] [On some sums involving small arithmetic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17474)
*Zhai Wenguang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究算术函数f的求和S_f(x)=∑_{n≤x} f([x/n])的渐近公式中的误差项E_f(x)。当f是"小"函数（即f(n)≤n^ε）时，误差项为O(x^{1/2+ε})。论文进一步研究了E_f(x)的均方性质，并针对一些特殊函数建立了新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数论中，形如S_f(x)=∑_{n≤x} f([x/n])的求和出现在许多算术函数的渐近分析中。已有研究表明当f是"小"函数时误差项为O(x^{1/2+ε})，但该误差项的精细性质（如均方估计）尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 论文首先推导E_f(x)的一般表达式，然后利用均值估计技术和Dirichlet双曲线法等方法研究其均方性质。针对特殊函数（可能包括除数函数、莫比乌斯函数等经典算术函数），论文采用了更精细的分析技巧来获得改进的误差估计。

Result: 建立了E_f(x)均方的一般性结果，并针对若干特殊算术函数得到了具体的改进估计，可能包括更优的指数或常数。

Conclusion: 该研究深化了对算术函数求和误差项的理解，特别是揭示了"小"函数假设下误差项的更精细结构，为相关数论问题的进一步研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $f$ be any arithmetic function and define $S_f(x):=\sum_{n\leq x}f([x/n])$. If the function $f$ is small, namely, $f(n)\ll n^\varepsilon,$ then the error term $E_f(x)$ in the asymptotic formula of $S_f(x)$ has the form $O(x^{1/2+\varepsilon}).$ In this paper, we shall study the mean square of $E_f(x)$ and establish some new results of $E_f(x)$ for some special functions.

</details>


### [108] [Betti-Whittaker periods under duality: variations and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17617)
*Giancarlo Castellano,Shih-Yu Chen,Nasit Darshan,A. Raghuram*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Betti-Whittaker周期在对偶下行为的结果从GL_n/ℚ推广到任意数域F上的GL_n，无需正则性假设和L-值的辅助，主要工具是GL_n的外自同构。并应用此结果于三重积L-函数、扭曲Asai L-函数和正交群L-函数的特殊值有理性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前Chen在正则性假设下并借助L-值证明了GL_n/ℚ上余调尖自守表示的Betti-Whittaker周期在对偶下的行为。本文旨在将该结果推广到任意数域F上的GL_n，去掉正则性假设，且不依赖L-值。

Method: 以GL_n的外自同构为主要工具，代替之前依赖L-值的证明方法；利用Harder和另一位作者(Raghuram)的结果给出应用。

Result: 得到了三重积L-函数和扭曲Asai L-函数特殊值比率的新的有理性质结果；给出了Bhagwat和Raghuram关于正交群L-函数特殊值结果的新证明；还给出了Betti-Shalika周期在对偶下和Betti-Whittaker周期在Galois自同构下行为的变体。

Conclusion: 成功将结果推广到任意数域F上的GL_n，无需正则性假设和L-值，给出了多个L-函数特殊值有理性的新结果，并提供了已有结果的新证明。

Abstract: One of the authors (Chen) had previously proved a result on the behavior of Betti-Whittaker periods under duality for cohomological cuspidal automorphic representations of ${\rm GL}_n/{\mathbb Q}$ under some regularity assumptions while using their relation to $L$-values as an anchor in his proof. In this article we prove a generalization of this result to ${\rm GL}_n$ over any number field $F$ without any regularity assumptions and without recourse to $L$-values, while using the outer-automorphism of ${\rm GL}_n$ as the main tool. Then, using results of Harder and one of the other authors (Raghuram), we give applications to new rationality results for the ratios of special values of general triple product $L$-functions and for general twisted Asai $L$-functions. We also give a new proof of a previous result of Bhagwat and Raghuram on the special values of $L$-functions for orthogonal groups. We present variations on period relations for the Betti-Shalika periods under duality, and the behavior of Betti-Whittaker periods under Galois automorphisms of $F$.

</details>


### [109] [Jacob's ladders and new $ζ$-functionals and corresponding $ζ$-equivalents of the Fermat-Wiles theory based on sums of elementary $ζ$-pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17731)
*Jan Moser*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在黎曼假设下，提出了基于孤立矩形脉冲信号之和的新型ζ等价形式，这是对费马-威尔士定理（即费马大定理）的zeta函数等价变体的求和变体研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展此前关于费马-威尔士定理的ζ等价形式的研究，探索其求和变体，特别是在黎曼假设成立的前提下，寻求新的等价表达形式。

Method: 在黎曼假设条件下，通过构造孤立矩形信号（脉冲）的和，推导出新型的ζ等价形式。

Result: 成功获得基于孤立矩形脉冲信号之和的新型ζ等价形式。

Conclusion: 在黎曼假设下，存在基于矩形脉冲信号之和的新的ζ等价变体，丰富了费马-威尔士定理与ζ函数之间的等价关系。

Abstract: In this paper we obtain some sum-variants of our first $ζ$-equivalent of the Fermat-Wiles theorem. For example, on the Riemann hypothesis, we give a new type of $ζ$-equivalent based on a sum of isolated rectangle-shaped signals (pulses).

</details>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [A Centrality Measure Using Magnitude Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16377)
*Nadja Häusermann,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文基于量同调（magnitude homology）理论，提出了一种新的图节点中心性度量方法，该方法通过移除顶点时量同调的变化来衡量节点重要性，并验证了其满足多种合理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将度量空间的量（magnitude）这一表达性不变量与图论中的节点中心性相结合，利用量同调理论开发一种新的局部中心性度量指标。

Method: 受相对同调概念启发，通过移除图中某个顶点前后量同调的变化来定义该节点的中心性/重要性。

Result: 提出的中心性度量满足多项合理的性质，并与多种已有的中心性度量进行了比较，展示了新的视角。

Conclusion: 基于量同调的中心性度量是一种有效的图节点重要性评估方法，拓展了中心性研究的新方向。

Abstract: The magnitude of a metric space constitutes an expressive invariant that subsumes numerous different geometrical-topological invariants. Building on recent advances in magnitude homology, i.e., a bigraded homology theory that recovers the magnitude, we develop a novel local measure of the centrality or importance of nodes in a graph. Our measure is inspired by the concept of relative homology as it considers the change in magnitude homology when removing a vertex. We show that our proposed measure satisfies several properties a centrality measure is reasonably expected to respect and demonstrate that we introduce a new perspective on centrality by comparing to several established centrality measures.

</details>


### [111] [On the wreath transfer in the homology of symmetric groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17114)
*Dezhou Li*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文对奇素数p，给出了与标准包含Σ_p≀Σ_p ≤ Σ_{p²}相关的花环转移tr: H_*(Σ_{p²}; F_p) → H_*(Σ_p≀Σ_p; F_p)的显式公式。证明采用直接代数计算，结合Cartan经典双陪集公式与生成函数论证。作为直接推论，获得了Goodwillie演算中混合Adem关系有效性的初等证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为花环转移建立显式计算公式，进而为Goodwillie演算中的混合Adem关系提供初等证明。

Method: 直接代数计算，使用Cartan双陪集公式和生成函数论证。

Result: 得到了花环转移tr的显式公式。

Conclusion: 作为推论，给出了Goodwillie演算中混合Adem关系有效性的初等证明。

Abstract: For an odd prime $p$, we give an explicit formula for the wreath transfer $\mathrm{tr} \colon H_*(Σ_{p^2};\mathbb{F}_p)\longrightarrow H_*(Σ_p\wrΣ_p;\mathbb{F}_p)$ associated with the standard inclusion $Σ_p\wrΣ_p\leΣ_{p^2}$. The proof is a direct algebraic computation, using Cartan's classical double-coset formula together with a generating-function argument. As an immediate consequence, we obtain an elementary proof of the validity of the mixed Adem relations in Goodwillie calculus.

</details>


### [112] [Classification of Finite Subquandles in Spherical Quandles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17137)
*Kentaro Yonemura,Naoki Nakamura*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 球面quandle的每个有限子quandle都由二面体quandle和根系上定义的quandle所涵盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面quandle的有限子quandle的结构，并找出它们与二面体quandle和根系quandle之间的关系。

Method: 通过代数结构和几何表示理论，分析球面quandle的有限子quandle，并将其分类到二面体quandle和根系quandle中。

Result: 证明了球面quandle的所有有限子quandle都可以由二面体quandle和根系quandle来表示。

Conclusion: 球面quandle的有限子quandle结构完全由二面体quandle和根系quandle刻画。

Abstract: Every finite subquandle of a spherical quandle is accounted for by dihedral quandles and by quandles defined on root systems.

</details>


### [113] [The Euler Characteristic of Finite Subset Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17830)
*Walid Taamallah*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文针对拓扑空间X，研究了其有限子集空间Sub_n X（由X中基数不超过n的非空子集构成）的欧拉示性数，给出了当X具有有限CW复形同伦型时的显式公式：χ(Sub_n X) = ∑_{i=1}^n C(χ(X), i)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算拓扑空间X的有限子集空间Sub_n X的欧拉示性数，建立其与X本身欧拉示性数之间的显式关系。

Method: 利用代数拓扑和组合拓扑的方法，针对具有有限CW复形同伦型的空间X，推导出Sub_n X的欧拉示性数公式。

Result: 得到了显式公式 χ(Sub_n X) = ∑_{i=1}^n C(χ(X), i)，其中C表示组合数。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了有限子集空间欧拉示性数与原始空间欧拉示性数之间的简洁组合关系，为这类空间的拓扑不变量计算提供了有效工具。

Abstract: For a topological space $X$, the space of finite subsets $\mathrm{Sub}_n X$ consists of non-empty subsets of $X$ of cardinality at most $n$. We compute the Euler characteristic of $\mathrm{Sub}_n X$ for any space $X$ having the homotopy type of a finite CW complex. We obtain the explicit formula $χ(\mathrm{Sub}_n X) = \sum_{i=1}^n \binom{χ(X)}{i}$.

</details>


### [114] [Periodic phenomena in stable motivic homotopy theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18165)
*Jackson Morris*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文综述了稳定同伦论工具在动机同伦论中的应用与影响，重点讨论了动机Adams谱序列、球面动机稳定同伦群的周期性以及合成谱，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理稳定同伦论工具如何影响并推动动机同伦论的发展，为研究者提供该交叉领域的全景式综述。

Method: 通过综述性研究，整合并讨论动机Adams谱序列、球面动机稳定同伦群的周期性规律以及合成谱理论等核心内容。

Result: 总结了稳定同伦论工具在动机同伦论中的具体表现形态和影响，包括多种动机Adams谱序列的构造、球面动机稳定同伦群中发现的周期性模式，以及合成谱在该领域的应用。

Conclusion: 动机同伦论深受稳定同伦论工具的影响，但仍有许多开放问题值得进一步探索。

Abstract: In this survey, we study how tools from stable homotopy theory have manifested and impacted motivic homotopy theory. In particular, we discuss various motivic Adams spectral sequences, periodicity in the motivic stable homotopy groups of spheres, and synthetic spectra. We conclude with many problems for future investigation.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Evolutionary dynamics in public goods games with general frequency-dependent returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16809)
*Qiushuang Wang,Yuyuan Liu,Xiaojie Chen,Attila Szolnoki*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了公共物品博弈中频率依赖的乘数因子对合作演化的影响，通过理论分析推导出合作优势的条件，并扩展到带有同伴惩罚的博弈中，发现合作或惩罚的丰度仅取决于群体全贡献或全惩罚状态下的投资回报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公共物品博弈通常假设乘数因子为常数，但现实中投资回报往往随群体策略组成动态变化，而这一频率依赖情形下的演化动力学尚未被充分理解。

Method: 引入依赖于群体策略组成的频率依赖乘数因子，通过理论分析推导合作被偏好的数学条件，并通过个体基模拟验证理论结果。同时将该因子扩展到带有同伴惩罚的公共物品博弈中。

Result: 合作是否具有演化优势取决于全贡献状态下的投资回报率，与其他状态无关；该回报率越高，合作者丰度越高。在惩罚博弈中，合作者丰度仅由全合作状态回报率决定，惩罚者丰度仅由全惩罚状态回报率决定。

Conclusion: 频率依赖的投资回报对合作和惩罚的演化有显著影响，全贡献和全惩罚状态下的回报率分别独立调控合作与惩罚行为的涌现。

Abstract: The public goods game serves as a significant paradigm for investigating the emergence and maintenance of cooperation in conflicting situations. In the traditional public goods game, the multiplication factor characterizing the synergy effect of common efforts is typically assumed to be constant. In real-world scenarios, however, investment returns are often dynamic and vary with the strategic composition of the interaction group. To date, the evolutionary dynamics of the public goods game with such frequency-dependent returns have remained not fully understood. In this work, we introduce a general frequency-dependent multiplication factor that depends on the strategy composition within the game group. Through theoretical analysis, we derive the mathematical conditions under which cooperation is favored. Our results show that whether cooperation has an evolutionary advantage over defection depends on the investment return rate in the full-contribution state of the game group, irrespective of the return rates in other states. An increase in this rate leads to a higher abundance of cooperators. Furthermore, we introduce a general frequency-dependent multiplication factor into the public goods game with peer punishment, and systematically explore its effects on the cooperation dilemma and the second-order free-rider problem. Our results highlight that the abundance of cooperators or punishers is governed solely by the investment return values in the full-cooperation and full-punishment compositions of the group. A higher return rate in the full-cooperation state facilitates the promotion of cooperation, whereas a higher return rate in the full-punishment state favors the emergence of punishment. Our theoretical findings are verified by individual-based simulations.

</details>


### [116] [How genome redundancy can promote evolutionary innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17687)
*Domenico Caudo,Mattia Miotto,Giancarlo Ruocco,Greta Grassmann*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 通过最小进化模型研究了多倍体在不同选择压力下与遗传模式和基因型-表型映射的相互作用，发现多倍体在环境剧变时尤为有利，随机遗传结合非线性映射最大化表型开发和景观探索，而结构化遗传结合加性映射在低至中等倍性水平出现最优分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多倍体在生物界广泛存在，但其究竟是进化创新还是进化死胡同仍有争议，本文旨在探究不同选择压力下倍性水平如何与遗传模式和基因型-表型映射相互作用。

Method: 构建最小进化模型，系统分析不同选择压力下，遗传模式（随机/结构化）与基因型-表型映射（非线性最大/加性）在不同倍性水平下的演化效果。

Result: 随机遗传结合最大映射在所有环境中最大化表型开发和景观探索；结构化遗传结合加性映射表现较差但在低至中等倍性出现最优；多倍体在环境剧变时尤为有利；自由演化时种群倍性由开发、探索和收敛速度三者权衡决定。

Conclusion: 多倍体的适应性取决于遗传模式、基因型-表型映射与选择压力的协同作用，提供了一个理解自然选择何时及为何偏好多倍体的统一框架。

Abstract: Polyploidy is defined as the existence of more than two complete sets of homologous chromosomes. Despite it being a widespread phenomenon across the tree of life, its role as either an evolutionary innovation or a dead end is still debated. Here, we investigate how under varying selective pressures the degree of ploidy interacts with two key biological factors: the mode of inheritance and the genotype-phenotype mapping. Through a minimal evolutionary model we find that polyploidy is especially advantageous during abrupt environmental changes, confirming that polyploidization is often associated with ecological upheavals. We observe that stochastic inheritance combined with a nonlinear (maximum-based) genotype-phenotype mapping maximizes both phenotypic exploitation and landscape exploration across all environments. By contrast, structured inheritance with an additive phenotype mapping systematically underperforms, yet displays a pronounced optimum at low-to-intermediate ploidy level that mirrors the distribution observed in natural plant and bacterial populations. When individuals are free to carry different chromosome numbers, selection drives the population toward values that reflect an interplay between exploitation, exploration, and convergence speed rather than any single evolutionary objective. The relative weight of these three factors depends on the fitness landscape, providing a unifying framework for understanding when and why polyploidy is favored by natural selection.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [HyperDCM: Dynamic Cluster Memory Replay in Hyperbolic Space for Continual Robotic Navigation Across Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16267)
*Zhengfei Lu,Jian Yang,Muyu Wang,Shaowen Chen,Jinpeng Mi,Ke Li,Xiong You,Qi Wu,Xuan Tang,Xian Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperDCM，一种基于双曲动态聚类的结构感知记忆机制，通过场景图建模和记忆重放增强扩散策略导航的持续学习能力，在室内外多场景数据上优于现有持续学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉导航中的持续学习面临灾难性遗忘和适应多样化动态环境的困难。

Method: 利用视觉-语言模型从RGB观测中提取语义场景三元组，通过R-GCN编码为场景图嵌入，再投影到双曲空间以增强结构可分离性；采用动态聚类和结构敏感更新策略选择代表性样本进行记忆重放，保持知识多样性并缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在多场景室内外数据集上，HyperDCM相比适配于扩散策略导航的持续学习基线，在保留已有导航能力和提升泛化性方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: HyperDCM通过结构感知的双曲记忆机制有效缓解了持续视觉导航中的灾难性遗忘问题，提升了模型在多场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Continual learning in visual navigation remains challenging due to catastrophic forgetting and the difficulties associated with adapting to diverse and evolving environments. To address these issues, we propose Hyperbolic Dynamic Cluster Memory (HyperDCM), a structure-aware memory mechanism that enhances diffusion policy-based navigation through scene graph modeling and principled memory replay. HyperDCM extracts semantic scene triples from RGB observations using large vision-language models, encodes them into scene graph embeddings via a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (R-GCN), and projects the embeddings into hyperbolic space to enhance structural separability and retention in continual navigation. A dynamic clustering and structure-sensitive update strategy selects representative samples for memory replay, thereby preserving knowledge diversity and mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Experiments on multi-scene indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate that HyperDCM achieves superior retention of past navigation capabilities and improved generalization compared to representative continual learning baselines adapted to diffusion policy navigation.

</details>


### [118] [Control Design for a Rideable Animatronic Two-Wheeled Robot with Quadruped Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16302)
*Tadashi Sumioka,Kazushi Akimoto,Takuya Tsujimura,Sho Takayanagi,Yuya Horiuchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种可骑行的四足双轮机器人，通过自平衡两轮基座实现主要运动，四肢提供辅助支撑并协调运动，以在保持角色忠实度的同时实现动态拟人化运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来摩托车在年轻群体中的受欢迎程度下降，为重新激发这一群体对摩托车的兴趣，开发一种可作为未来伙伴出行概念的可骑行双轮机器人，用于公共活动展示。

Method: 机器人采用自平衡双轮基座作为主要运动方式，四肢在上下车时提供辅助支撑，并与轮式运动速度协调配合。研究重点包括：在快速肢体运动时保持稳定的鲁棒自平衡控制方法，以及生成自然四足行为的运动控制策略。

Result: 实现了能够通过直觉式重心偏移控制进行自由自然运动的动画机器人，在快速肢体运动时仍能保持稳定性，并展现出动态的四足步态行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的自平衡双轮基座结合辅助四肢的方案，有效解决了纯粹依靠肢体运动所需的高扭矩、大重量和极慢步态等问题，在保证角色忠实度和骑乘安全性的同时，实现了富有表现力的机器人运动。

Abstract: In recent years, motorcycle popularity has been declining, particularly among the younger generations. To rekindle interest in motorcycles among this demographic, we developed a rideable two-wheeled robot equipped with four limbs as a future partner mobility concept and intended for use in public events. The character reproduced by this robot carries the protagonist on its back and exhibits a dynamic quadrupedal gait. To maximize the riding experience, we aimed to match the robot's weight and size to the character's specifications while ensuring rider safety and enabling expressive movements of limbs, wrists, ankles, and facial features. However, achieving locomotion solely through limb movement would require excessive motor output and increased limb strength, resulting in higher weight and extremely slow gait, thereby reducing character fidelity. To overcome these challenges, the robot performs its primary locomotion using a self-balancing two-wheeled base, while the limbs provide auxiliary support during mounting and dismounting and move in coordination with the wheeled locomotion speed. This approach enables an animatronic robot capable of intuitive, weight-shift-based control for free and natural movement. In this paper, we focus on a robust selfbalancing control method that maintains stability even during rapid limb movements, as well as a motion control strategy that generates natural quadruped-like behavior.

</details>


### [119] [Real-Time sEMG-Based Telecontrol of an Assistive Robotic Arm Using a 1D Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16310)
*Edgar Manacorda,Mena Samir Kama Abouseffien,Olivier Lecompte,Amandine Gesta,Abolfazl Mohebbi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表面肌电图(sEMG)的实时接口，用于辅助机器臂的遥操作。系统采用四通道sEMG采集、信号预处理、滑动窗口分割和一维卷积神经网络(CNN)分类，在仿真和真实机器人平台上实现了90%以上的测试准确率和约0.32秒的平均延迟，证明了sEMG遥控制用于辅助机器人的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上肢运动障碍严重影响日常生活自主性，尤其涉及物体操作的任务。辅助机器臂提供了一种有前景的解决方案，但需要直观、可靠且响应迅速的控制方式。sEMG作为一种非侵入式人机接口，能够获取肌肉活动及用户运动意图，因此被选为控制信号来源。

Method: 系统采用四通道sEMG采集，进行信号预处理和滑动窗口分割；分类使用一维卷积神经网络(CNN)；研究了多种实时策略，包括阈值启停检测、两级分类（静息vs运动+手势识别）以及单分类器同时处理静息和五种手势；系统在仿真和真实机器人平台上进行了评估。

Result: CNN方法实现了90%以上的测试准确率，对实验采集信号具有良好泛化能力；系统实时行为稳定，平均延迟约0.32秒；在仿真和真实环境中均可通过离散手势实现可靠、连贯平滑的机器臂控制。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于sEMG的遥控制用于辅助机器人是可行的，强调了在统一实时框架中整合信号处理、深度学习与控制策略的重要性。未来可探索sEMG与额外传感模态结合的混合控制方法以进一步提升鲁棒性和可用性。

Abstract: Motor impairments affecting the upper limb significantly reduce autonomy in daily activities, particularly for tasks involving object manipulation. Assistive robotic arms offer a promising solution, provided they can be controlled in an intuitive, reliable, and responsive manner. Among human--machine interface approaches, surface electromyography (sEMG) enables non-invasive access to muscle activity and thus to the user's motor intentions. This work proposes a real-time sEMG-based interface for the teleoperation of an assistive robotic arm. The system relies on four-channel sEMG acquisition, signal preprocessing, segmentation into sliding windows, and classification using a one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN). Several real-time strategies are investigated, including threshold-based onset detection, a two-stage classification approach (rest vs movement followed by gesture recognition), and a single classifier handling both rest and five gestures. The complete pipeline is implemented and evaluated both in simulation and on a real robotic platform. The CNN-based approach achieves high classification performance, with a test accuracy above 90\% and strong generalization on experimentally acquired signals. The system exhibits stable real-time behavior, with an average latency of approximately 0.32 s consistent with the chosen windowing strategy, and the robot can be controlled reliably using discrete gestures, producing coherent and smooth movements in both simulated and real environments. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of sEMG-based telecontrol for assistive robotics and highlight the importance of integrating signal processing, deep learning, and control strategies within a unified real-time framework. Future work may explore hybrid control approaches combining sEMG with additional sensing modalities to further improve robustness and usability.

</details>


### [120] [S.E.A.G.R: A Socially and Emotionally Aware Greeting Robot Framework with Dual-Layer Cultural and Affective Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16341)
*Sajjad Hussain,Ranveer Bhura,Amandip Dutta,Shwetangshu Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SEAGR（社交与情感感知问候机器人）框架，通过双层调制机制（文化身份决定问候类型，情感线索调节执行方式），结合上下文感知文化映射、基于情感的姿势调节和人际距离规约，在Sense-Think-Act统一架构下实现面向多元文化背景和不同情绪状态用户的适应性机器人问候交互。目前作为系统设计与概念验证，缺乏用户研究的实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共空间机器人仍依赖静态问候程序，未能考虑文化差异、情感上下文和人际距离，导致交互缺乏社会适应性和得体性，影响第一印象、用户舒适度和信任建立。

Method: SEAGR采用双层调制框架：上层由文化身份映射决定问候类型（如鞠躬、握手、挥手等），下层由情感线索（面部表情、语音情感）调节问候的执行方式（如幅度、速度、距离）。系统基于Sense-Think-Act架构整合文化映射、情感姿态调节和人际距离规约，原型使用USB摄像头、超声波传感器、Arduino控制舵机和基于Python的笔记本电脑处理系统实现。

Result: 提出并实现了SEAGR系统原型，作为一个低成本的社交机器人问候框架，证明了双层调制机制在技术上的可行性。目前仍为概念验证阶段，缺乏用户实验的实证数据支持。

Conclusion: SEAGR为跨文化、情感感知的机器人问候交互提供了系统化的设计框架，解决了现有系统静态固定的问题。当前主要局限是缺乏用户研究的实证验证，这被明确列为未来工作的首要方向。

Abstract: This paper presents SEAGR (Socially and Emotionally Aware Greeting Robot), a robotic greeting framework designed for human-robot interaction environments involving users from diverse cultural backgrounds and different emotional states. Since greeting behaviour strongly influences first impressions, user comfort, and trust, robots operating in public spaces must be able to interact in a socially appropriate and adaptive manner. However, many existing systems still rely on static greeting routines that do not account for cultural variation, emotional context, or interpersonal distance. SEAGR introduces a dual-layer modulation framework in which cultural identity determines the appropriate greeting type, while affective cues influence how that greeting is executed. The system combines context-aware cultural mapping, emotion-based gesture modulation, and proxemic regulation within a unified Sense-Think-Act architecture. A low-cost prototype is implemented using a USB camera, ultrasonic sensor, Arduino-controlled servos, and a laptop-based Python processing system. This work is presented as a system design and proof-of-concept; empirical validation through user studies is explicitly acknowledged as a current limitation and is identified as the primary direction for future work.

</details>


### [121] [PRISM: Multimodal Terrain Mapping for Rover Navigation in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16366)
*Raul Castilla-Arquillo,Carlos Perez-del-Pulgar,Levin Gerdes,Alfonso Garcia-Cerezo,Miguel A. Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一种面向非结构化环境的 multimodal 感知系统，通过融合RGB、深度和热成像数据（RGB-D-T），利用基于视觉Transformer的OmniUnet网络进行地形语义分割，并在资源受限的嵌入式平台上生成可通行性地图，实现自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的机器人导航需要强大的态势感知能力来安全穿越陡坡、岩石地形等障碍，而现有感知系统在融合多模态数据方面仍存在不足，尤其是热成像与光学+深度传感器的有效融合可以显著提升地形区分能力和建图可靠性。

Method: 提出PRISM系统，包含定制传感器套件用于对齐RGB、深度和热成像（RGB-D-T）数据；核心是OmniUnet，一种基于视觉Transformer的多模态语义地形分割网络；在两个新标注数据集（BASEPROD和LAENTIEC）上验证，并在资源受限的嵌入式计算机上部署运行。

Result: 通过两个新标注数据集的验证和实际物理场实验，证明了PRISM在非结构化环境中生成可通行性地图的有效性，并且能够在嵌入式平台上高效运行，直接用于无人车的制导、导航与控制（GNC）子系统。

Conclusion: PRISM通过RGB-D-T多模态融合和OmniUnet网络，成功实现了非结构化环境下的高效地形感知与可通行性地图生成，为资源受限平台上的自主导航提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic navigation in unstructured environments requires robust situational awareness to safely traverse hazards such as steep slopes and rocky terrain. To address this challenge, perception systems increasingly rely on multimodal sensor fusion. Specifically, integrating thermal imagery with standard optical and depth sensors enhances terrain differentiation, directly improving the reliability of mapping algorithms. This paper presents PRISM, a multimodal perception system for terrain mapping in unstructured settings. PRISM leverages a custom sensor suite to capture aligned RGB, depth, and thermal (RGB-D-T) imagery. At its core is OmniUnet, a novel vision transformer-based network specifically designed for multimodal semantic terrain segmentation. We validated the proposed system using two newly annotated datasets (BASEPROD and LAENTIEC) and demonstrate its real-world applicability through physical field experiments. Deployed on a resource-constrained embedded computer, PRISM efficiently generates traversability maps that directly enable autonomous navigation via a rover's Guidance, Navigation, and Control (GNC) subsystem.

</details>


### [122] [Back to the museum: Investigation of the acceptance of Android Andrea with and without emotion simulation in a museum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16428)
*Marcel Heisler,Christian Becker-Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 安卓机器人Andrea在德国博物馆进行了6天全自主多语种对话实验，通过三种情感条件（无情感、ChatGPT 4.1驱动情感、WASABI架构模拟情感）测试73名访客的主观评价，结果表明前两种情感实现方式未产生积极效果且未被访客有意识地察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在先前定性研究基础上，探索在真实公共场景中为高度类人但性别模糊的安卓机器人引入情感表达能力是否会影响访客对其的主观评价和接受度。

Method: 在德国博物馆部署安卓机器人Andrea 6天，实现全自主多语种对话；采用三种情感条件（无情感、ChatGPT 4.1驱动情感、WASABI架构模拟情感）；使用扩展版TAM2问卷收集73名访客的定量评价数据并进行统计分析。

Result: 前两种情感实现方式（无情感、ChatGPT 4.1驱动情感）未对访客的主观评价产生任何积极效果，且访客在意识层面未能察觉到情感差异。

Conclusion: 当前两种情感实现方式在安卓机器人上的应用未能提升用户体验，暗示简单的情感模拟策略可能不足以在高度类人机器人中产生可感知的积极影响。

Abstract: For a second time, the android robot Andrea was set up at a public museum in Germany for six consecutive days to have conversations with visitors, fully autonomously. Building on previously gathered qualitative results, the robot was now capable of engaging in multi-lingual conversation with the visitors about the museum context. The robot was prepared with context information about the museum in general and its surrounding exhibits this time. The robot featured a slightly artificial sounding voice that was previously evaluated as congruent with its gender-ambiguous but very humanlike design. Three experimental conditions were implemented, in which either (1) the robot simulated no emotions, (2) the robots emotions were determined by ChatGPT 4.1, or (3) the WASABI emotion simulation architecture simulated the robot's emotion dynamics. An extended version of the TAM2 questionnaire was employed to let 73 visitors report on several factors of their opinion about the android robot Andrea after having experienced it. In result, the statistical analysis suggests that these first two approaches to implementing emotions into the chat architecture of our android robot Andrea did not yield any positive effects on the subjective evaluations by the visitors and were not detectable on a conscious level.

</details>


### [123] [Approximate Relative Entropy Constraints for Nonlinear Covariance Steering Under Distribution Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16914)
*Trevor N. Wolf,Jay W. McMahon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性系统中协方差 steering 在局部线性化导致的分布近似误差问题，提出了基于相对熵(KLD)的分布鲁棒协方差 steering 框架，通过推导风险敏感量的上界并控制真实分布与高斯参考之间的 KLD 变化率，开发了序列凸规划算法，在近直线晕轨道转移中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差 steering 框架在非线性系统中依赖局部线性化得到的高斯近似，该近似与真实非线性状态分布可能存在显著差异，导致碰撞概率、均方误差等风险敏感量估计不准确。

Method: 基于 KLD 构建分布鲁棒协方差 steering 框架；利用指数积分的变分表示推导 KLD 模糊集上风险敏感量的可计算上界；推导真实非线性分布与高斯参考之间 KLD 时间变化率的上界，并将其与决策变量关联；将约束纳入序列凸规划算法。

Result: 在近直线晕轨道之间的非线性航天器转移场景中验证了所提方法的有效性，表明该方法能使真实分布接近高斯参考并满足风险敏感性能约束。

Conclusion: 通过分布鲁棒方法有效弥补了非线性系统中协方差 steering 因线性化近似带来的分布偏差问题，提高了风险敏感性能指标的估计可靠性。

Abstract: Covariance steering provides an efficient framework for designing linear stochastic feedback policies, but its extension to nonlinear systems relies on a Gaussian surrogate obtained through local linearization. Because this surrogate may differ substantially from the true nonlinear state distribution, risk-sensitive quantities such as collision probability and mean-squared error may be inaccurately estimated. This work develops a distributionally robust covariance-steering framework based on the relative entropy, also known as the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KLD), to account for ambiguity in the propagated probability density function. Using a variational representation of exponential integrals, we derive computable upper bounds on risk-sensitive quantities over a KLD ambiguity set. We then formulate an upper bound on the time rate of change of the KLD between the true nonlinear distribution and a Gaussian reference surrogate. Under some assumptions, this bound is controlled by decision variables within a covariance-steering formulation. The resulting constraints are incorporated into a sequential convex programming algorithm to design stochastic guidance policies that keep the true distribution close to its Gaussian surrogate while enforcing bounds on risk-sensitive performance measures. The proposed approach is demonstrated on a challenging nonlinear spacecraft transfer between two near-rectilinear halo orbits.

</details>


### [124] [Linear Stability Analysis of an INDI Pitch-Rate Controller under Model Mismatch for a Tilt-Rotor VTOL UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16471)
*Lorenzo Schenk,Guillaume Ducard,Christopher Onder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对倾转旋翼VTOL无人机的增量非线性动态反演（INDI）控制，首次以参数显式方式解析表征了内环模型失配的稳定边界。通过推导俯仰率/升降副翼子通道的五阶闭环传递函数，利用Routh-Hurwitz准则生成三维参数扫描的稳定区域图，并据此提出鲁棒导向和性能导向两种不确定性感知调参策略。研究发现：执行器延迟和惯性失配影响较小，而控制效能失配（特别是符号错误）是最危险的失稳因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾转旋翼VTOL无人机的INDI控制虽然能减少对完整气动模型的依赖，但其内环容许的模型失配范围尚未以参数显式方式得到解析表征，缺乏理论指导的调参依据。

Method: 1) 隔离级联INDI控制器中的俯仰率/升降副翼子通道；2) 推导控制器-估计器-执行器-被控对象互联的闭式五阶传递函数；3) 基于参数化线性模型，利用Routh-Hurwitz准则进行稳定性分析；4) 进行三维参数扫描生成可解释的稳定区域图；5) 提出鲁棒导向（最大化加权最坏情况增益裕度和相位裕度）和性能导向（最大化最坏情况闭环带宽并满足裕度约束）两种调参流程。

Result: 执行器延迟和惯性失配在名义增益下影响较小；控制效能失配（尤其是分配符号错误）是最危险的失稳因素，并据此给出了保守和激进工况下的具体调参建议。

Conclusion: 本文为倾转旋翼VTOL无人机的INDI控制器提供了解析化的稳定性边界分析工具，揭示了不同参数失配的失稳风险等级，并给出了实用的不确定性感知调参策略。

Abstract: Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) is attractive for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight control because it reduces dependence on a full aerodynamic model while retaining strong disturbance-rejection capability. For a tilt-rotor vertical takeoff and landing (VTOL) architecture, however, the admissible model-mismatch range of the fast inner loop is still not characterized analytically in a parameter-explicit way. This paper isolates the pitch-rate/elevon subchannel of an existing cascaded INDI controller and studies its linear stability under model mismatch. A closed-form fifth-order transfer function is derived for the full controller-estimator-actuator-plant interconnection, and stability is characterized through the Routh-Hurwitz criterion over a parameterized linear model. Two representative three-parameter sweeps produce interpretable stability regions. Based on these feasibility maps, two uncertainty-aware tuning procedures are proposed: a robustness-oriented design that maximizes a weighted worst-case combination of gain margin and phase margin, and a performance-oriented design that maximizes worst-case closed-loop bandwidth subject to margin constraints. The results show that actuator lag and inertia mismatch are comparatively benign at nominal gain, whereas control-effectiveness mismatch, particularly a sign error in the allocation, is the most dangerous destabilizing factor, leading to concrete tuning recommendations for conservative and aggressive operating conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Certifiable Safe Model-Based Reinforcement Learning with Control-Affine Dynamics Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16501)
*Hao Zhou,Yanze Zhang,Cameron Reid,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的安全强化学习框架，通过控制仿射随机傅里叶特征（ARFF）学习控制仿射动力学，并利用自适应共形预测（ACP）量化不确定性，从而在数据驱动下实现可认证的安全策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法往往忽视残差模型不确定性的结构（如与控制输入仿射的分量），导致机器人行为过于保守或安全保证失效。

Method: 1) 使用控制仿射随机傅里叶特征（ARFF）建模控制仿射形式的机器人动力学，提升计算效率并降低模型偏差；2) 采用自适应共形预测（ACP）对学习到的控制仿射动力学中的安全约束进行无模型不确定性量化，实现数据驱动的安全保证。

Result: 在cartpole和3D四旋翼平台上的仿真结果验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过学习控制仿射动力学并量化其不确定性，结合CBF合成控制器，可在保证安全的前提下实现高效的任务执行，避免过度保守行为。

Abstract: Safe model-based reinforcement learning (RL) often bridges control-theoretic analysis and RL for robots to safely explore (partially) unknown system dynamics while deriving control actions for task efficiency. The control performance and safety assurance typically rely on prior knowledge of partially modeled nominal system dynamics and the data-driven models that compensate for residual model uncertainties. However, existing methods often overlook the structure of residual model uncertainties (e.g., components affine in control), which could lead to overly conservative robot behaviors or invalid safety guarantees under the safe learning-based controllers. This paper proposes a safe reinforcement learning framework that learns control-affine dynamics with a certifiable data-driven safe policy using control barrier functions (CBF). Specifically, we first use Control-Affine Random Fourier Features (ARFF) to model robot dynamics in a control-affine form, which offers computational efficiency that scales with dataset size and reduces potential model bias for model-based reinforcement learning. Then, a model-free, efficient uncertainty quantification method using adaptive conformal prediction (ACP) is applied to quantify the uncertainty in the safety constraint arising from the learned control-affine dynamics. This allows for data-driven safety assurance amenable to principled and efficient controller synthesis with CBF. Simulation results on the cartpole and the 3D quadrotor platforms demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [126] [Foresight Residual RL for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16506)
*Yuhan Liu,Xinyu Zhang,Litao Liu,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略在紧公差、高接触装配任务中因子任务耦合和信用分配问题导致的长程任务失败，本文提出Foresight Residual RL方法，通过离线估计的前瞻价值（未来子任务成功概率）作为奖励乘数来优化子任务间的交接质量，在螺母拧紧任务中达到85.6%的完整任务成功率，远超基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在通用操作任务中表现优异，但在紧公差、高接触的装配任务（如螺母拧紧）中频繁失败。根本原因在于子任务间的耦合与长程信用分配问题：当前子任务在几何上成功的末端状态可能对后续子任务而言是脆弱的。标准残差强化学习虽然能提高各子任务独立完成率，但串联后性能几乎没有提升，因为终端状态质量不受控制。

Method: 提出Foresight Residual RL，包含两个关键组件：(i) 训练一个视觉前瞻预测器，从基础策略的末端状态图像出发，利用下游 rollout 统计标记，预测未来子任务成功的概率；(ii) 通过逆向前瞻归纳训练残差策略，将预测器输出作为稀疏成功奖励的乘数，从而优化子任务交接时的末端状态质量。

Result: 在Isaac Gym中三阶段（抓取-移动插入-旋转）的扳手式螺母拧紧装配任务上，Foresight Residual RL达到85.6%的完整任务成功率，显著优于标准子任务残差RL（54.5%）以及VLA基线方法，同时各子任务独立成功率保持不变。

Conclusion: 提升长程操作性能的关键不仅在于每个子任务是否成功，更在于塑造每个子任务产生的成功状态的具体质量。通过前瞻价值引导残差策略优化交接状态，可以有效解决子任务耦合导致的长程失败问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies offer strong general-purpose manipulation priors, but often fail on tight-tolerance, contact-rich assembly due to long-horizon credit assignment and subtask coupling: a state that is geometrically successful for the current skill can be brittle for downstream skills. We show this failure mode in residual reinforcement learning (RL) over a frozen VLA base policy: constant sparse success rewards improve each subtask in isolation yet yield little or no gain when skills are chained, because terminal state quality is uncontrolled. We propose Foresight Residual RL, which optimizes handoff quality by augmenting each subtask's sparse success reward with an offline-estimated foresight value -- the probability of future subtask success conditioned on the terminal state of the current subtask. Concretely, we (i) train a visual foresight predictor from images of terminal states of the base policy, labeled using downstream rollout statistics, and (ii) train residual policies via backward foresight induction, using the predictor output as a reward multiplier. On a three-phase wrench-based nut-tightening assembly task in Isaac Gym (grasp, move-insert, rotate), our method achieves 85.6% full-task success, outperforming standard subtask residual RL (54.5%) and VLA baselines, while leaving per-subtask success unchanged. These results highlight that improving long-horizon performance requires shaping which successful states are produced at each sub-task, not only whether success occurs.

</details>


### [127] [Differentiable Reinforcement Learning for Path Tracking by an Agile Fish-Like Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16508)
*Prashanth Chivkula,Kartik Loya,Venkata Ravindhra Reddy Varikuti,Phanindra Tallapragada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个高效仿真平台，结合PID控制与反向传播时间训练法，使仿生鱼机器人实现了从仿真到实物的成功迁移控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿生鱼机器人因流固耦合建模困难和非线性欠驱动动力学特性，控制与运动规划极具挑战性；现有强化学习方法因缺乏兼具计算速度和精度的仿真环境而难以应用于鱼形机器人。

Method: 开发了一个计算高效的仿真平台近似鱼形机器人运动；采用PID控制进行运动控制和路径跟踪，其中（可变）增益通过反向传播时间法和课程训练进行学习。

Result: 在仿真中学习的策略成功迁移到物理平台，且展现出极佳的匹配度。

Conclusion: 所提出的仿真平台结合学习型PID控制方法能够有效解决仿生鱼机器人的控制难题，并实现仿真到实物的零差距迁移。

Abstract: Fish-like swimming has inspired the design of several dozens if not hundreds of bioinspired robots in the last few decades. But the control and motion planning of such robots has been challenging due to the poorly modeled fluid-structure interaction and the nonlinear underactuated dynamics of such robots. While reinforcement learning has allowed significant advances in the context of ground and aerial robots, the lack of a suitable simulation environment with appropriate computational speed and accuracy have prevented similar progress for fish-like robots. We address this two-fold problem by developing a simulation platform that approximates the motion of our fish-like robot with computational efficiency. Then the motion control and path tracking by the robot is performed using PID control where the (variable) gains are learned using back propagation through time and training on a curriculum. The policy learned in the simulation is then applied on the physical platform, demonstrating an excellent match.

</details>


### [128] [Autonomous VR-Based Risk Detection for Situational Awareness in Dangerous Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16582)
*Mohammad Eskandari,Murali Krishna Varma Indukuri,Stephanie M. Lukin,Cynthia Matuszek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于VR的人机交互框架，利用VLM辅助机器人识别危险并在沉浸式界面中标注给操作员，实验表明该方式提升了情景意识且用户偏好度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险环境（如灾难响应）中，情景意识不仅依赖于检测危险，还需要将这些危险清晰传达给人类操作员。VLM在安全关键场景理解方面潜力巨大，但其作为面向人类的机器人系统组成部分的价值尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了一个VR人机交互框架：机器人探索虚拟场景，通过VLM识别潜在危险并标注用户感兴趣的点，这些标注通过沉浸式VR界面呈现给人类操作员。通过对比实验评估有标注与无标注基线下的用户体验。

Result: 用户更偏好带标注的VR界面；参与者报告了高清晰度、有用性和舒适性。VLM加上VR可视化能有效支持危险环境中的情景意识。

Conclusion: 将VLM驱动的机器人感知与沉浸式可视化相结合是支持危险环境情景意识的一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: In high-risk environments such as disaster response, situational awareness depends not only on detecting hazards but also on communicating them clearly to human operators. Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for scene understanding in safety-critical settings, yet their value as part of human-facing robotic systems remains underexplored. We present a VR-based Human Robot Interaction framework for studying how VLM-assisted robots can support situational awareness in simulated hazardous environments. In our system, a robot explores a virtual scene and queries a VLM to identify potential hazards and annotate user-facing points of interest. These annotations are presented to a human operator through an immersive VR interface. This framework enables controlled evaluation of both robotic hazard identification and the communication of safety-critical information to users. Results from our study indicate that the annotated VR interface was preferred over the unannotated baseline and that participants reported high clarity, usefulness, and comfort when interacting with the system. These findings suggest that combining VLM-based robotic perception with immersive visualization is a promising approach for supporting situational awareness in hazardous settings.

</details>


### [129] [An Indoor Navigation System for the Visually Impaired based on UWB Positioning and D* Lite Path Planning Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16614)
*Thanh C. Vo,Dong LT. Tran,Huy HM. Le,Duyen N Ha,Tuan Anh Pham,Hai Thanh Dang,Hoang T. Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于UWB定位技术和D* Lite路径规划算法的室内导航系统，专为视障人士设计，能在GPS受限环境中实现高精度定位与动态避障，实验验证了系统的低延迟与高可靠性。


<details>
