### [196] [A family of mixed-mixed strongly conservative finite element methods for Biot's model of consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16418)
*Qingguo Hong,Johannes Kraus,Maria Lymbery*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Biot准静态固结模型提出了一种四场变分形式（弹性应力、固体位移、流体通量、流体压力），证明了两重扰动鞍点问题的适定性，并基于H(div div)协调有限元提出了一族混合-混合有限元方法，实现了角动量和流体质量守恒的逐点满足，给出了最优先验误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Biot固结模型四场形式的严格数学框架，并设计能在逐点（强形式）意义上同时保持角动量守恒和流体质量守恒的数值离散方法。

Method: 采用四场变分公式将Biot模型转化为两重扰动鞍点问题；在新型Hilbert空间中证明位移-通量对的适定性；基于H(div div)协调有限元构造混合-混合有限元格式。

Result: 证明了连续问题和离散问题的适定性，建立了完全保守的混合-混合有限元方法族的最优先验误差估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在离散层面逐点保持角动量和流体质量平衡，且具有最优收敛阶。

Abstract: We consider a four-field formulation of Biot's quasi-static model of consolidation with the (effective) elastic stress, the solid displacement, the fluid flux and the fluid pressure as unknown physical quantities of interest. The weak form of this system composed of the momentum and mass balance equations complemented by the stress-strain relation and Darcy's law yields a two-fold perturbed saddle-point problem. Its well-posedness is proven for a natural choice of inifinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces, where the displacement-flux pair is sought in a novel Hilbert space. Next, based on recent works on $H(\rm div \, \rm div)$-conforming finite elements, we propose a family of mixed-mixed finite element methods that preserve the angular momentum as well as the fluid mass balance pointwise, i.e., in a strong sense. We prove the well-posedness of the arising discrete problem and establish optimal a priori error estimates for the related family of fully conservative mixed-mixed finite element methods.

</details>


### [197] [Convergence and stability of truncated Euler-Maruyama algorithm for stochastic proportional delay Mckean-Vlasov models with jump process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16438)
*Amr Abosenna,Zhuoqi Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对带Lévy跳的随机比例延迟McKean-Vlasov模型，在非跳跃系数允许超线性增长的条件下，应用截断Euler-Maruyama算法，研究了该数值算法的收敛速度和几乎必然指数稳定性，并通过数值算例验证了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机McKean-Vlasov模型在金融、生物学和控制等领域具有重要应用，但现有研究对非跳跃系数超线性增长情形下的数值算法分析尚不充分，亟需建立相应的收敛性和稳定性理论。

Method: 采用截断Euler-Maruyama算法处理带Lévy跳的随机比例延迟McKean-Vlasov模型，在非跳跃系数允许超线性增长条件下，推导算法的收敛速度并分析几乎必然指数稳定性。

Result: 给出了所提截断Euler-Maruyama算法的收敛速度估计，并建立了算法几乎必然指数稳定性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 截断Euler-Maruyama算法能够有效处理系数超线性增长的随机比例延迟McKean-Vlasov模型，且具有良好的收敛性和稳定性，数值实验支持了理论结果。

Abstract: Stochastic Mckean-Vlasov models have a substantial importance in different fields such as finance, biology and control. This paper puts the light on stochastic proportional delay Mckean-Vlasov model with Lévy jump where the non-jump coefficients are granted the permission to grow beyond linearity. The truncated Euler-Maruyama algorithm is then applied to our addressed model where the convergence rate and almost sure exponential stability of the aforementioned numerical algorithm are being investigated. Finally, numerical examples are presented to foster the theoretical analysis done throughout the paper

</details>


### [198] [Surrogate-to-code verification of a non-intrusive POD-GPR machine-learning emulator of peak thermomechanical fields, with application to a carbon-carbon aircraft brake disc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16447)
*Franklin Kamche*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种验证框架，用于评估非侵入式降阶代理模型相对于有限元求解器自身离散化不确定性的预测精度。以碳-碳飞机刹车盘在中断起飞场景为例，利用POD-GPR管道预测峰值温度和von Mises应力。结果表明，在独立测试集上，代理模型与高保真求解器的预测值在其离散化不确定度内一致，且残差主要源于线性降维限制而非回归误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证级应用需要严格评估代理模型相对于求解器自身离散化不确定性的误差，而非依赖物理观测数据。现有方法缺乏直接针对数值不确定性底线的误差评估框架。

Method: 提出代数精确的后验误差预算，将代理模型的有符号误差分解为截断分量和回归分量，并通过网格收敛研究建立离散化底线，最终定义“代理模型-代码保真度”基准。

Result: 在独立测试集上，代理模型预测的峰值温度和峰值von Mises应力均在高保真求解器的离散化不确定度范围内；残差主要来自POD线性降维截断而非GPR回归误差。

Conclusion: 该验证框架可用于任何可获得离散化误差估计的参数化有限元模拟的非侵入式降阶模型，并额外贡献了热-力峰值并发预测和极值空间区域定位的概率方法。

Abstract: While non-intrusive reduced-order emulators successfully substitute for costly finite-element simulations, certification-grade applications demand a rigorous error assessment relative to the solver's own discretization uncertainty. This work introduces a verification framework designed to evaluate prediction accuracy directly against this numerical uncertainty floor, rather than physical observation. We demonstrate this approach on a carbon-carbon aircraft brake disc during a rejected take-off scenario, predicting peak temperature and peak von Mises stress fields. Operating over a fixed mesh of 60914 nodes, the emulator relies on a pipeline coupling proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) and Gaussian process regression (GPR) parameterized by 5 inputs. We formulate an algebraically exact, a posteriori error budget that isolates the emulator's signed error into distinct truncation and regression components at the extremum. This error is then evaluated against a discretization floor established via mesh-convergence studies on the peak quantities of interest. On a completely independent test set, our emulator matches the high-fidelity solver's peak values within its own discretization uncertainty for both fields, a benchmark we define as surrogate-to-code fidelity. Notably, the residual error stems primarily from linear reduction limits rather than regression inaccuracies. Beyond the main framework, we present two secondary contributions: the concurrent, non-intrusive prediction of the thermal and mechanical peaks, and a probabilistic approach to spatial zone localization for these extrema. This methodology applies to non-intrusive reduced-order models of parameterized finite-element simulations wherever a discretization-error estimate for the quantity of interest can be obtained.

</details>


### [199] [Finite element exponential integration for rough solutions of nonlinear wave equations. Part I: Dirichlet boundary conditions on polygonal and polyhedral domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16511)
*Jiachuan Cao,Benjamin Dörich,Marlis Hochbruck,Buyang Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界多边形/多面体域上非线性波动方程的全离散格式，结合指数欧拉时间积分器和有限元空间离散，针对低正则性解建立了严格的误差估计，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低正则性误差分析主要基于傅里叶谱离散化，适用于周期域或特殊几何，缺乏对一般有界域和有限元空间离散化的处理。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用指数欧拉时间积分器与有限元空间离散相结合的全离散格式；利用椭圆算子的频率分解作为解析正则化工具（非实际实现），将低正则性技术推广到傅里叶谱框架之外。

Result: 建立了低正则性解的严格误差估计；证明了高阶有限元方法在低Sobolev正则性解的空间逼近中仍具优势；数值实验在不同域和不同多项式次数下验证了收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该全离散格式可有效处理一般有界域上非线性波动方程的低正则性解，且高阶有限元法在低正则性情形下仍具优势。

Abstract: We study a fully discrete scheme for nonlinear wave equations on general bounded polygonal/polyhedral domains with initial data $(u^0,v^0)\in H^γ(Ω)\times H^{γ-1}(Ω)$, $0<γ\le 1$, subject to the natural compatibility condition associated with the homogeneous Dirichlet boundary condition. The scheme combines an exponential Euler time integrator with a finite element spatial discretization. In contrast to existing low-regularity error analyses, which are mostly based on Fourier spectral discretizations, our approach applies to general bounded domains and finite element spatial discretizations. We prove rigorous error estimates for low-regularity solutions. The analysis is based on a frequency decomposition of the underlying elliptic operator, used solely as an analytical regularization device and not in the actual implementation, which allows low-regularity techniques to be extended beyond the Fourier spectral framework. The results also indicate that higher-order finite element methods remain advantageous in spatial approximation even for solutions of limited Sobolev regularity. Numerical experiments on different domains and with different polynomial degrees confirm the predicted convergence behavior.

</details>


### [200] [Augmented Lagrangian preconditioning for a simplified Ericksen--Leslie model of nematic liquid crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16628)
*Yanying Li,Xu Qian,Jingmin Xia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对简化Ericksen-Leslie液晶模型中强耦合、不可压缩性与单位长度约束并存的数值挑战，开发了一种增强拉格朗日块预条件子，通过双约束增广和物理尺度化Schur补近似，实现了近网格无关的FGMRES迭代收敛，并验证了主变量的空间精度与一阶时间收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化Ericksen-Leslie液晶模型的数值求解面临三大挑战：流动与指向场方程强耦合、不可压缩性约束与单位长度约束必须同时满足。现有方法中，Ginzburg-Landau小参数方法存在局限性，而拉格朗日乘子法则导致Newton系统呈现双鞍点结构，求解困难。

Method: 采用拉格朗日乘子公式避免小Ginzburg-Landau参数；开发增强拉格朗日块预条件子，对两个约束均进行增广且保持乘子基离散；有限元离散+向后欧拉时间积分；将Newton增量分组为速度-指向变量和压力-乘子变量；通过速度-指向块的块对角近似，构造压力和指向-乘子Schur补的物理尺度化近似。

Result: 制造解测试显示主变量达到预期空间精度和一阶时间收敛；乘子误差在固定网格上呈现空间误差平台；在报告参数范围内，外层FGMRES迭代次数几乎与网格无关，对时间步长和粘度变化保持稳定，并随增广参数增大而改善；光滑基准测试中计算总能量单调衰减。

Conclusion: 开发的增强拉格朗日块预条件子有效解决了简化Ericksen-Leslie模型的数值求解难题，在精度、收敛性和稳定性方面均表现良好。

Abstract: The numerical solution of the simplified Ericksen--Leslie model for nematic liquid crystals is challenging because the flow and director equations are strongly coupled and because incompressibility and the unit-length condition must be enforced simultaneously. A Lagrange multiplier formulation avoids a small Ginzburg--Landau parameter, but the Newton systems have a double saddle-point structure. We develop an augmented Lagrangian block preconditioner in which both constraints are augmented while their discrete enforcement remains multiplier based. After finite element discretization and backward Euler time integration, the Newton increments are grouped into velocity--director and pressure-multiplier variables. A block-diagonal approximation of the coupled velocity-director block then leads to separate, physically scaled approximations of the pressure and director-multiplier Schur complements. Manufactured-solution tests show the expected spatial accuracy and first-order temporal convergence for the primary variables; the multiplier error reaches a spatial-error floor on the fixed mesh used in the temporal study. In the reported parameter ranges, the outer FGMRES iteration counts are nearly mesh independent, remain stable under time-step and viscosity variation, and improve as the augmentation parameters increase. A smooth benchmark also exhibits monotone decay of the computed total energy.

</details>


### [201] [A Deep Second-Order Stochastic Residual Method for Fully Nonlinear Parabolic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16730)
*Zhenhua Zhao,Jihao Long*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了深度二阶随机残差方法（D2SRM），用于求解高维、依赖于Hessian矩阵的全非线性抛物型偏微分方程。该方法通过单个标量时空网络生成解、梯度和Hessian的一致近似，并利用二阶布朗单步残差、终端值和梯度惩罚进行联合训练。在全局Lipschitz方程和足够弱的Hessian耦合条件下，建立了布朗占据空间中的适定性和总体收敛理论。实验表明，在100维问题上，随着时间步长减小，误差持续下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维全非线性抛物型PDE的数值求解极具挑战性，尤其是当方程显式依赖于解的二阶导数（Hessian矩阵）时，传统方法（如有限差分、有限元）受维度灾难限制，而现有神经网络方法多针对半线性或一阶方程，难以处理Hessian依赖项。

Method: D2SRM使用单个标量时空神经网络同时近似解、梯度和Hessian，通过二阶布朗运动驱动的单步残差损失（结合终端值和梯度惩罚）进行联合训练；理论分析在布朗占据空间框架下进行，利用小增益条件建立适定性和误差界。

Result: 在全局Lipschitz且Hessian耦合足够弱的条件下，建立了布朗占据空间中的适定性；后验误差界将全喷射占据误差与时间步长和总体目标函数关联；当神经逼近误差和总体次优性均为O(h)时，全喷射占据范数为O(h^{1/2})；100维基准实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: D2SRM为求解高维Hessian依赖型全非线性抛物型PDE提供了一种可证明收敛的深度学习方法，在理论和实验上均表现出色，且代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce the Deep Second-Order Stochastic Residual Method (D2SRM) for high-dimensional, Hessian-dependent fully nonlinear parabolic PDEs. A single scalar space--time network generates derivative-consistent approximations of the solution, gradient, and Hessian, which are trained jointly through second-order Brownian one-step residuals and terminal value and gradient penalties. For globally Lipschitz equations with identity diffusion and sufficiently weak Hessian coupling, we establish well-posedness in a Brownian occupation space and develop a population-level convergence theory. Under additional regularity, an a posteriori estimate bounds the squared full-jet occupation error of any admissible candidate by the time step and its population objective. For approximate population minimizers, the error bound separates time discretization, neural approximation, and population suboptimality; when the latter two terms are $O(h)$, the full-jet occupation norm is $O(h^{1/2})$. Experiments on a 100-dimensional manufactured benchmark compare terminal treatments, probe Hessian couplings inside and outside the proved small-gain range, and show decreasing errors as the time step decreases. The code is available at https://github.com/ZZHPKU/D2SRM.

</details>


### [202] [Analytic regularity for a fourth-order singularly perturbed boundary balue problem with two small parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16781)
*I. Sykopetritou,C. Xenophontos*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维双参数四阶奇异摄动边值问题，在解析输入数据假设下，将解分解为光滑部分、两种不同宽度的边界层和可忽略余项，并给出了各分解项任意阶导数的显式估计（明确依赖于微分阶数和摄动参数），同时证明了解在解析数据下的解析性及微分时出现的负幂次现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高阶数值方法（如有限元法的p/hp版本）的收敛性证明提供必要的解的正则性估计，特别是任意阶导数的显式界，以处理双参数奇异摄动问题中的边界层效应。

Method: 在解析输入数据假设下，通过渐近展开技术将解分解为光滑部分、两种不同宽度的边界层成分和可忽略余项，并利用解析函数理论推导各分量任意阶导数的显式估计。

Result: 给出了分解中各分量任意阶导数的显式估计，该估计明确依赖于微分阶数和两个奇异摄动参数；同时证明了在解析数据下解本身是解析的，但微分时会出现摄动参数的负幂次。

Conclusion: 所获得的显式导数估计为高阶数值方法（如p/hp有限元法）的收敛性分析提供了理论基础，揭示了双参数奇异摄动问题的解析正则性结构。

Abstract: We consider a fourth order singularly perturbed boundary value problem with two small parameters, in one dimension, under the assumption of analytic input data. We show that the solution may be decomposed into a smooth part, two different width boundary layers, and a negligible remainder. We provide estimates for arbitrary order derivatives of each term of the decomposition, which are explicit in the differentiation order and the singular perturbation parameters, and are needed for proving the convergence of high order numerical methods, such as the $p/hp$ versions of the Finite Element Method. We also provide classical differentiability results, which show that the solution will be analytic, if the data are analytic, but negative powers of the singular perturbation parameter(s) show up once we start differentiating.

</details>


### [203] [PolyChopper: a Polyhedron Splitting Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16887)
*Tommaso Sorgente,Fabio Vicini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: PolyChopper是一种用于将凸多面体分割为有限个不相交凸子多面体的新方案，通过平面切割、顶点调整及引入楔形体、四面体和金字塔等元素，递归生成层次化的高质量四面体化细分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸多面体分割方法在生成子多面体的质量和复杂度控制方面存在不足，需要一种既能保证凸性又能在元素质量和复杂度之间灵活权衡的鲁棒分割方案。

Method: 算法使用平面切割凸多面体（平面可外部指定或由惯性张量自动确定），调整切割产生的交点位置以控制元素质量和复杂度，根据用户定义的质量参数引入额外的"切口楔形体"、四面体和金字塔，递归应用该算法可生成层次化细分。

Result: 实验表明该方法鲁棒性强，持续生成比原始多面体更简单、质量更高的子多面体；递归应用可生成元素逐渐缩小、保持凸性且以四面体为主的层次化细分结构。

Conclusion: PolyChopper提供了一种鲁棒、可控的凸多面体分割方法，能够递归生成高质量、以四面体为主的层次化凸细分，在元素质量和复杂度之间实现了有效平衡。

Abstract: We introduce and implement a novel scheme, called \textit{PolyChopper}, for splitting a convex polyhedron into a finite set of disjoint convex sub-polyhedra. The algorithm cuts the polyhedron with a plane, which can either be prescribed as an external constraint or automatically determined from the inertia tensor. The resulting intersection vertices are then adjusted to control the complexity and quality of the generated elements, introducing additional ``notch wedges'', tetrahedra and pyramids, according to a user-defined quality parameter. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach is robust and consistently produces sub-polyhedra that are both simpler and of higher quality than the original polyhedron. Furthermore, recursively applying the algorithm generates a hierarchy of polyhedral subdivisions with progressively smaller elements, all guaranteed to remain convex and predominantly tetrahedral.

</details>


### [204] [Neural operator preconditioning from mixed dataset for the Helmholtz equations: Application to transcranial ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16906)
*Yanfei Xiang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种神经算子预条件子子空间方法，用于求解经颅超声应用中二维Helmholtz方程离散化产生的序列线性系统。该方法在粗网格上训练神经算子，并将其作为非线性预条件子集成到FGMRES中，能够在训练网格64倍大的网格上高效求解实际问题，而经典GMRES和学习优化器均无法在可比计算预算内收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经颅超声应用中强异质性、依赖于患者的速度场会导致严重的波畸变，给标准迭代求解器带来巨大挑战，亟需开发高效且具有泛化能力的求解方法。

Method: 基于Giraud等人的神经网络预条件框架和Stanziola等人的理想化颅骨数据集，在粗网格上使用基于离散Helmholtz方程相对残差的物理信息损失训练神经算子预条件子，并将其作为非线性预条件子集成到柔性GMRES（FGMRES）中。采用混合数据集策略，将随机化源配置与基于理想化颅骨的速度场及随机噪声相结合，共构建六个混合速度-源数据集进行训练。

Result: 所提出的混合方法能够在训练网格64倍大的网格上高效求解经颅超声问题，而经典GMRES和学习优化器均无法在可比计算预算内收敛。该方法可实现任意求解精度，并在不同的源和速度配置下展现出强分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文凸显了数据集设计在科学机器学习中的重要性，并提供了一个将无矩阵神经算子预条件与Krylov子空间方法集成的实用框架，用于求解实际大规模Helmholtz问题。

Abstract: This work develops a neural operator preconditioned subspace method for sequences of linear systems arising from the discretization of the two-dimensional Helmholtz equation in transcranial ultrasound applications. The problem involves strongly heterogeneous, patient-dependent velocity fields that induce severe wave distortion and pose significant challenges for standard iterative solvers. Building on neural network preconditioning framework of Giraud et al. (HAL RR-9593, 2025) and the idealized skull dataset used for the learned optimizer of Stanziola et al. (JCP 441, 2021), neural operator preconditioners are trained on six mixed velocity-source datasets combining randomized source configurations and idealized skull-based velocity fields with random noise. The proposed mixed-dataset strategy aims to improve both computational efficiency and generalization across varying configurations. The neural operator is trained on a coarse grid using a physics-informed loss based on the relative residual of the discrete Helmholtz equation and is incorporated as a nonlinear preconditioner within flexible GMRES (FGMRES). Numerical experiments demonstrate that the resulting hybrid method efficiently solves practical transcranial ultrasound problems on grids 64 times larger than those used during training, whereas both classical GMRES and the learned optimizer fail to converge within comparable computational budgets. Moreover, the proposed method achieves arbitrary solution accuracies and exhibits strong out-of-distribution generalization across diverse source and velocity configurations. This work highlights the importance of dataset design in scientific machine learning and provides a practical framework for integrating matrix-free neural operator preconditioning with Krylov subspace methods for solving practical large-scale Helmholtz problems.

</details>


### [205] [Novel Adaptive Methods for Hyperbolic Conservation Laws Based on New Quasi-Linear Seventh- and Ninth-Order Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16918)
*Shaoshuai Chu,Pingyao Feng,Vadim A. Kolotilov,Alexander Kurganov,Vladimir V. Ostapenko*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文开发了用于一维和二维双曲守恒律系统的自适应数值格式，通过在光滑区域使用七阶和九阶拟线性有限差分格式，相比五阶格式减少了数值耗散并提高了分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在开发比现有五阶QL方案数值耗散更小、分辨率更高的自适应数值格式，以提高双曲守恒律系统的计算精度。

Method: 基于光滑度指示器将计算域自动划分为光滑和非光滑区域，在光滑区域采用新的七阶和九阶拟线性有限差分格式，非光滑区域采用原有方案，形成自适应数值格式。

Result: 通过一系列欧拉方程数值实验表明，新自适应方案相比使用五阶QL方案的自适应方案，数值耗散更小，分辨率更高。

Conclusion: 高阶QL方案在光滑区域的应用能有效提升自适应格式的整体性能，减少耗散并提高分辨率。

Abstract: We develop new adaptive numerical schemes for one- and two-dimensional hyperbolic systems of conservation laws. The methodology relies on the use of a smoothness indicator to automatically partition the computational domain into smooth and nonsmooth (``rough``) regions. We then follow the scheme adaption strategy recently introduced in [S. Chu, P. Feng, V. A. Kolotilov, A. Kurganov, and V. V. Ostapenko, Commun. Comput. Phys., accepted], but instead of the quasi-linear (QL) fifth-order finite-difference scheme used there, we employ the new QL seventh- and ninth-order schemes in the smooth regions. A series of numerical experiments for the Euler equations of gas dynamics demonstrates that the new adaptive schemes contain a smaller amount of numerical dissipation and achieve higher resolution compared with their counterpart that uses the QL fifth-order scheme in the smooth areas.

</details>


### [206] [A Linear Variable-Step Embedded ETD Scheme with Uniform-in-Time Stability for the 2D Navier--Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17036)
*Haifeng Wang,Xiaoming Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于二维不可压Navier-Stokes方程（涡量-流函数形式）的线性变步长指数时间差分方法，包含二阶格式和一阶嵌入格式，支持自适应时间步长和后验误差控制，每步仅需求解线性问题，并证明了无条件长时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不可压Navier-Stokes方程的高效数值模拟，现有ETD方法或mr-SAV方法存在非线性求解或稳定性不足的问题，需要一种兼具线性性、长时间稳定性和自适应步长能力的数值格式。

Method: 结合指数时间差分(ETD)框架、均值回归标量辅助变量(mr-SAV)和非线性项的二阶外推；采用二阶格式与嵌入一阶格式实现自适应步长；每个时间步求解两个热方程（傅里叶方法高效处理）和一个线性SAV方程（Laplace变换+Talbot数值逆变换）。

Result: 证明了无条件长时间稳定性：对于一致有界L²外力，离散涡量在L^∞(0,∞;L²)中有界，对所有Reynolds数和时间步长均成立。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持完全线性的同时实现了长时间稳定性，与需要非线性代数求解的mr-SAV方案形成鲜明对比，数值实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We propose a linear variable-step exponential time-differencing method for the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations in vorticity--streamfunction formulation on a two-dimensional periodic box. The method consists of a second-order scheme and an embedded first-order variant, yielding a natural mechanism for adaptive time stepping and a posteriori error control. Each time step requires only uniquely solvable linear problems: two heat equation solves, efficiently handled by Fourier methods in the periodic setting, and one linear scalar auxiliary-variable equation, evaluated via Laplace transform and Talbot's numerical inverse transform.
  The construction combines the ETD framework, a mean-reverting scalar auxiliary variable (mr-SAV), and second-order extrapolation of the nonlinear term. The mean-reverting correction enables long-time stability while preserving full linearity, distinguishing the method from related mr-SAV schemes that require nonlinear algebraic solves. We prove unconditional long-time stability: for uniformly bounded $L^2$ forcing, the discrete vorticity remains bounded in $L^\infty(0,\infty;L^2)$ for all Reynolds numbers and time-step sizes. Numerical experiments

</details>


### [207] [Convergence of Finite Element Methods for Ricci Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17051)
*Guangwei Gao,Evan S. Gawlik,Buyang Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维Ricci流有限元离散格式的收敛性，该方法将Ricci流转化为解驱动度量演化，通过有限元离散并保持面积守恒和高斯-博内定理等几何结构，数值实验验证了收敛性和模拟效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维Ricci流建立具有收敛性保证且能保持重要几何结构的数值离散方法。

Method: 将Ricci流重新表述为解驱动度量演化形式，其中高斯曲率满足依赖于度量的抛物型方程；采用有限元方法离散，并借鉴外蕴曲率流中用于研究解驱动曲面演化的矩阵-向量公式来证明收敛性。

Result: 证明了有限元离散格式的收敛性，并验证了该方法在离散层面保持面积守恒和高斯-博内定理；数值实验展示了收敛性和Ricci流模拟效果。

Conclusion: 提出的有限元方法为二维Ricci流提供了收敛性保证的数值方案，同时保持了关键几何结构，具有良好的实际模拟能力。

Abstract: The convergence of a finite element discretization for the two-dimensional Ricci flow is proved. In this method, the Ricci flow on a two-dimensional surface is formulated into solution-driven metric evolution, with the metric evolution driven by the Gauss curvature. The Gauss curvature satisfies a parabolic equation that in turn depends on the metric, thereby enhancing the parabolic structure of the problem. The solution-driven metric evolution formulation is discretized by the finite element method, and the convergence of finite element approximations is proved by adapting the matrix-vector formulation developed in the literature initially for studying solution-driven surface evolution in extrinsic curvature flow. In addition to its convergence, the proposed method also preserves important geometric structures of the Ricci flow at the discrete level, such as area conservation and the Gauss-Bonnet theorem. Extensive numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the convergence of the proposed method as well as the simulation of Ricci flow.

</details>


### [208] [Digital Nets on Cubature Nodes: Inheriting Cubature Accuracy on Low-Dimensional Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17080)
*Takehito Yoshiki*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种坐标嵌入方法，将低维超方格规则的高精度插入到高维数字网络（digital nets）中，无需构造全张量积，从而显著提升有限预算下高维积分的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基-2数字网络是高维积分的实用规则，但其光滑低维投影分量的精度远不如同等节点数的低维超方格规则。因此需要一种方法，在不形成全张量积的前提下，将低维超方格精度嵌入高维数字网络规则中。

Method: 采用简单的坐标嵌入方法：读取每个坐标的前p个二进制位作为索引，映射到2^p个等权重超方格节点，并用该节点替换坐标值。当投影形成完整的p位网格时，变换后的规则在该投影上等价于对应的乘积超方格规则；小的投影t值提供全网格恢复的充分条件。

Result: 误差可分解为对应的乘积超方格误差和残差数字网络项。在50维的Sobol'网实验表明，该方法对光滑低阶和坐标衰减测试函数在有限预算下具有显著精度提升。

Conclusion: 提出的坐标嵌入方法成功将低维超方格精度注入高维数字网络，且实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Base-2 digital nets are practical high-dimensional integration rules: the sample budget $N=2^m$ can be chosen independently of the ambient dimension, and the generating matrices provide algebraic control of projections and Walsh-dual weights. They are therefore well suited to problems whose error is governed by weighted or low-dimensional projection structure. However, when one restricts attention to a smooth low-dimensional projected component, a low-dimensional cubature rule with a comparable number of nodes can be substantially more accurate than the projected digital-net points. This raises the question of whether low-dimensional cubature accuracy can be inserted into a high-dimensional digital-net rule without forming the full tensor product.
  We answer this question by a simple coordinate embedding: read the leading $p$ binary digits of each coordinate as an index into $2^p$ equal-weight cubature nodes, and replace the coordinate by the indexed node. When a projection forms the full $p$-bit grid, the transformed rule coincides on that projection with the corresponding product cubature rule; small projected $t$-values provide sufficient conditions for such full-grid recovery. For general integrands, the error separates into the corresponding product cubature error and a residual digital-net term. Experiments with scrambled Sobol' nets in dimension $50$ illustrate this mechanism and show finite-budget improvements for the smooth low-order and coordinate-decaying test functions considered here.

</details>


### [209] [A Lowest-Order Robust Mixed Finite Element Method with a Third-Order Tensor Variable for Strain Gradient Elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17134)
*Xuehai Huang,Zheqian Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对应变梯度弹性（SGE）模型，提出了一种任意维度的最低阶混合有限元方法。该方法以物理意义明确的三阶双应力张量作为主变量，采用最低阶Raviart-Thomas元逼近双应力，Crouzeix-Raviart元逼近位移，避免了高阶气泡富集和Nitsche型边界处理。建立了参数鲁棒的离散稳定性、固定参数下的最优一阶误差估计，以及参数一致的O(ι^{1/2}+h)误差估计。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变梯度弹性模型中存在高阶边界条件和小尺寸参数，传统有限元方法需要高阶气泡富集或Nitsche型边界处理，计算复杂且对参数敏感。本文旨在开发一种避免这些复杂处理的、参数鲁棒的低阶混合有限元方法。

Method: 将物理意义明确的三阶双应力张量Φ=ι²gradσ(u)∈S⊗R^d作为主变量，推导分布型混合变分形式。双应力用S⊗R^d值的最低阶Raviart-Thomas元逼近，位移用向量值线性Crouzeix-Raviart元逼近，无需高阶气泡富集和Nitsche型高阶边界条件处理。

Result: 建立了参数鲁棒的离散稳定性；固定参数下获得最优一阶误差估计；得到参数一致的O(ι^{1/2}+h)误差估计，常数独立于尺寸参数ι和Lamé系数λ；在边界层区域ι^{1/2}≲h时仍保持一阶收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提出的最低阶混合有限元方法有效避免了高阶气泡和Nitsche型处理，实现了参数鲁棒的稳定性与最优收敛性，并适用于二维和三维问题。

Abstract: A lowest-order mixed finite element method is developed for the strain gradient elasticity (SGE) model in arbitrary dimensions. We take the physically meaningful third-order double stress tensor $\boldsymbolΦ:=ι^2\operatorname{grad}\boldsymbolσ(\boldsymbol{u})\in\mathbb{S}\otimes\mathbb{R}^d$ as a primary variable and derive a distributional mixed formulation. The double stress is approximated by an $\mathbb{S}\otimes\mathbb{R}^d$-valued extension of the lowest-order Raviart--Thomas element, while the displacement is approximated by the vector-valued linear Crouzeix--Raviart element. Thus, the method avoids both high-degree bubble enrichment and a Nitsche-type treatment of the higher-order boundary condition. We establish parameter-robust discrete stability, an optimal first-order error estimate for fixed parameters, and a complementary parameter-uniform $\mathcal{O}(ι^{1/2}+h)$ error estimate with constants independent of both the size parameter $ι$ and the Lamé coefficient $λ$. In the boundary-layer regime $ι^{1/2}\lesssim h$, the latter retains a first-order convergence rate in $h$. We also develop a local quadratic post-processing and a hybridized formulation. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions support the theoretical results.

</details>


### [210] [A Rational Discrete Collocation Method for Second Kind Fredholm Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17179)
*Domenico Mezzanotte,Donatella Occorsio,Mario Pezzella,Woula Themistoclakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有理插值的离散配点法，用于在连续函数空间（一致范数）中数值求解第二类Fredholm积分方程。该方法利用无实极点、任意Jacobi节点插值且Lebesgue常数一致有界的有理近似，继承了其均匀收敛性（至少达到最佳多项式逼近的收敛速度）。在积分核的最小假设下，建立了稳定性、收敛性和良好条件。数值实验表明，对于具有挑战性的核，该方法比Nyström型方法更稳健有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对第二类Fredholm积分方程在一致范数下的数值求解，传统方法在处理某些具有挑战性的积分核时表现不佳，亟需一种更稳健且有效的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种基于再生核Hilbert空间框架下有理插值方案的离散配点法。该有理近似无实极点、在任意Jacobi节点插值且Lebesgue常数一致有界，将其引入配点法框架进行数值求解。

Result: 在积分核的最小假设下证明了方法的稳定性、收敛性和良好条件；数值实验表明在挑战性核的情况下，该方法优于传统的Nyström型方法。

Conclusion: 该方法继承了有理近似的优良性质，对所有连续函数具有一致收敛性（至少达到最佳多项式逼近的收敛速度），为求解第二类Fredholm积分方程提供了一种稳健有效的替代方案。

Abstract: In this work we present a novel discrete collocation method for the numerical solution of Fredholm integral equations of the second kind in the space of continuous functions equipped with the uniform norm. The method is based on a rational interpolation scheme recently developed within the general framework of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces. This rational approximation has no real poles, interpolates the target function at arbitrary Jacobi nodes and exhibits uniformly bounded Lebesgue constants. Moreover, it converges uniformly for all continuous functions at a rate at least equal to that of the best uniform polynomial approximation. These interesting properties are inherited by the resulting numerical method, for which stability, convergence and good conditioning are established under minimal assumptions on the integral kernel. A series of numerical experiments confirm the theoretical findings and indicate that, in the presence of particularly challenging kernels, the proposed approach provides a robust and effective alternative to Nyström-type methods.

</details>


### [211] [A Dynamical Approximation Scheme on the Stiefel manifold for Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17235)
*Isabella Carla Gonnella,Olga Mula,Federico Pichi,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种无网格拉格朗日动力学方法，通过将初始测度在加权希尔伯特空间中的传输映射投影到自适应构造的有限维子空间上，并利用Stiefel流形上的Dirac-Frenkel动力学原理演化正交标架，来近似Wasserstein梯度流；方法保证了能量耗散结构，并在测地凸能量下给出了后验误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Wasserstein梯度流数值方法（如JKO格式、欧拉方法）在高维问题或复杂能量泛函下计算成本高或精度受限，亟需一种高效、保结构、自适应且具有严格理论保证的近似方法。

Method: 将演化测度表示为初始测度μ₀通过传输映射的推前；传输映射在L²_{μ₀}的随时间变化的线性子空间中近似，其标准正交标架通过Stiefel流形上的Dirac-Frenkel动力学原理演化；子空间通过WGF速度场的局部近似自适应构造，约束在有限维背景空间中。

Result: 证明传输映射诱导Wasserstein空间中绝对连续的测度曲线，其速度为精确WGF速度在背景空间上的投影；近似保持能量耗散结构（误差仅来自速度投影）；对于测地凸能量，给出了后验误差估计，通过自适应背景空间控制投影误差，并得到Wasserstein度量下推前测度的近似误差界。数值实验验证了方法在Fokker-Planck方程、多孔介质扩散、相互作用能中的精度、能量耗散特性和自适应构造的优势。

Conclusion: 提出的无网格拉格朗日方法有效地近似了Wasserstein梯度流，兼具理论保障（保能量耗散、误差可控）和数值实用性（自适应、高精度），为求解WGF相关的高维和复杂问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a meshless Lagrangian dynamical method for approximating Wasserstein gradient flows (WGFs). The evolving measure is represented as the pushforward of the initial measure $μ_0$ through a transport map in the weighted Hilbert space $L^2_{μ_0}$. We approximate this map in time-dependent linear subspaces of $L^2_{μ_0}$, whose orthonormal frames are evolved by a Dirac--Frenkel dynamical principle on a Stiefel manifold constrained to a finite-dimensional background space, adaptively constructed via local approximations of the WGF velocity field. We prove that the resulting transport map induces an absolutely continuous curve of probability measures in Wasserstein space, whose velocity is obtained by projecting the exact WGF velocity onto the background space, and we show that the approximation preserves the energy dissipation structure up to the projection error of the velocity. Moreover, for geodesically convex energies, we derive an a posteriori estimate controlling such projection error through the adaptive construction of the background space, yielding as well a bound on the approximation error of the pushforward measure in the Wasserstein metric. Numerical experiments on linear and nonlinear Fokker--Planck equations, porous-medium diffusion, and interaction energies demonstrate the accuracy of the method, its energy-dissipation properties, and the advantages of the adaptive construction.

</details>


### [212] [Raviart--Thomas Elements with Geometric Correction for Distorted Quadrilateral Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17285)
*So-Hsiang Chou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对四边形网格上Raviart-Thomas空间因双线性映射破坏散度相容性的问题，本文提出在局部基函数中添加几何修正项，恢复相容性并保持原自由度与维数，理论分析与数值实验验证了最优逼近精度与改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Raviart-Thomas混合有限元在四边形网格上，由于参考单元到物理单元的双线性映射引入空间变化的Jacobian，导致散度相容性条件div V_h ⊂ W_h不再成立，从而影响方法的稳定性和精度。

Method: 对经典Raviart-Thomas基函数添加几何驱动的修正项，以补偿双线性映射带来的几何畸变，构建修正后的空间框架，并具体给出最低阶和次低阶修正Raviart-Thomas元素的构造。

Result: 理论分析在标准形状正则假设下建立了最优逼近性质；数值实验表明，在畸变网格上修正元素以预期收敛速率运行，且随几何畸变增大，修正格式的精度一致优于经典格式。

Conclusion: 所提修正方法简单有效，在保持Raviart-Thomas元素维数与自由度不变的前提下恢复相容性，显著提升了四边形网格上混合有限元方法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Mixed finite element methods based on Raviart--Thomas spaces are widely used for the numerical approximation of second--order elliptic problems in flux form. On quadrilateral meshes, however, the bilinear mapping from the reference element introduces a spatially varying Jacobian, which may violate the inclusion property $\mathrm{div}\,V_h \subset W_h$ for the standard Raviart--Thomas spaces.
  In this paper we propose a simple modification of the classical Raviart--Thomas elements on quadrilateral meshes. The modification consists of adding geometrically motivated correction terms to the local basis functions in order to compensate for the geometric distortion introduced by the bilinear mapping. The resulting spaces retain the same dimension and degrees of freedom as the classical Raviart--Thomas elements while restoring the compatibility property.
  We present a general framework for constructing such modified spaces and illustrate the approach by developing modified versions of the lowest order and next--to--lowest order Raviart--Thomas elements. Theoretical analysis establishes optimal approximation properties under the standard shape--regularity assumption for quadrilateral meshes. Numerical experiments on distorted meshes confirm the predicted convergence rates and show that the modified elements yield consistently improved accuracy over the classical Raviart--Thomas formulation as the geometric distortion increases.

</details>


### [213] [Stability and Robustness Analysis of Regularized Reconstruction Methods for Low-Dose Computed Tomography in Parallel-Beam Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17298)
*Mohamed Berrada*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了低剂量CT重建中FBP、Tikhonov正则化和TV最小化三种方法在多种噪声模型和稀疏采样条件下的稳定性与鲁棒性，提出稳定性因子S作为传统评价指标的补充，结果表明TV方法在噪声抑制、边缘保持和数值稳定性之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT在降低辐射暴露的同时引入了噪声和稀疏数据问题，使重建问题的不适定性加剧。现有正则化方法（如Tikhonov和TV）的性能高度依赖噪声特性、采样条件和参数选择，缺乏系统性稳定性与鲁棒性分析。

Method: 基于Radon变换构建2D平行束CT统一仿真流程，使用改进的Shepp-Logan模体和临床胸部图像，在180投影（基线）和60投影（稀疏视角）两种采样条件下，分别施加高斯、泊松及混合噪声，通过穷举SSIM网格搜索优化正则化参数，并引入经验性稳定性因子S量化从测量空间到图像空间的扰动放大程度。

Result: FBP对噪声和欠采样高度敏感；Tikhonov相比FBP改善了结构保真度，但对扰动的敏感性仍高于TV；TV在噪声抑制、边缘保持、准确性和数值稳定性之间提供了最佳折中。稳定性因子S能有效补充传统评价指标（RMSE、PSNR、SSIM）的信息。

Conclusion: 低剂量CT中存在稳定性与分辨率之间的权衡，所提出的稳定性因子S可作为传统度量指标的有价值补充，为LDCT重建方法的性能评估提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Low-dose computed tomography (LDCT) reduces radiation exposure but increases the ill-posedness of the reconstruction problem due to noise and sparse data. While regularized methods like Tikhonov and Total Variation (TV) improve image quality, their performance depends heavily on noise characteristics, sampling conditions, and parameter selection. This study presents a systematic stability and robustness analysis of Filtered Back Projection (FBP), Tikhonov regularization, and TV minimization within a 2D parallel-beam CT framework. A unified simulation pipeline based on the Radon transform is developed and evaluated using both the modified Shepp-Logan phantom and a clinical thorax image. Reconstruction behavior is investigated under multiple degradation scenarios involving Gaussian, Poisson, and mixed noise models, across baseline (180 projections) and sparse-view (60 projections) acquisition geometries. To ensure a fair comparison, regularization parameters are optimized for each scenario through an exhaustive SSIM-based grid-search. Quality is assessed via RMSE, PSNR, and SSIM, while robustness is quantified through an empirical Stability Factor S measuring perturbation amplification from measurement to image space. The results show that FBP is highly sensitive to noise and undersampling. Tikhonov regularization improves structural fidelity compared with FBP but remains more sensitive to perturbation than TV. Conversely, TV provides the best compromise between noise suppression, edge preservation, accuracy, and numerical stability. These findings highlight the stability-resolution trade-off in LDCT and demonstrate that the proposed Stability Factor S offers valuable complementary information to conventional metrics.

</details>


### [214] [Iterated graph Laplacian for image restoration problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17313)
*Stefano Aleotti,Davide Bianchi,Florian Bossmann,Marco Donatelli,Pietro Maurino*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究将图拉普拉斯算子作为广义Tikhonov框架中的正则化器，通过迭代更新拉普拉斯算子以融入更锐利的结构先验，提出了三种方案（标准、误差方程、混合），证明了其收敛性，并在CT和图像去模糊中验证了重建质量的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Tikhonov正则化使用固定正则化器，无法充分利用解的局部结构信息（如边缘、细节）。本文旨在通过迭代更新图拉普拉斯算子，使正则化项能够自适应地捕捉并强化解的锐利结构信息，从而提升不适定线性逆问题的重建质量。

Method: 提出了三种迭代更新图拉普拉斯的方案：(1) 标准方案：直接从当前重建解重构拉普拉斯；(2) 误差方程方案：基于迭代Tikhonov的误差公式，从重建误差估计中构建拉普拉斯；(3) 混合方案：结合前两者。在噪声水平趋于零时，基于先验参数和停止规则证明了三种方案的收敛性。

Result: 在二维CT和图像去模糊的数值实验中，三种方案均一致地提升了重建质量，并更锐利地恢复了精细细节。

Conclusion: 迭代更新图拉普拉斯算子能够有效提升Tikhonov正则化对线性不适定问题的求解效果，尤其是在保持边缘和恢复细节方面，且三种方案均具有理论收敛保证。

Abstract: We study the graph Laplacian operator as a regularizer in a generalized Tikhonov framework for linear ill-posed problems. The Laplacian is updated iteratively from the current reconstruction, so that progressively sharper structural information about the solution is fed into the regularization term. We introduce three schemes: a standard one that rebuilds the Laplacian from each new iterate; an error-equation scheme that, following the error-based formulation of iterated Tikhonov regularization, builds the Laplacian from an estimate of the reconstruction error rather than of the image itself; and a mixed scheme combining the two. We establish convergence of all three schemes for noisy data under a priori parameter and stopping rules as the noise level tends to zero. Numerical experiments in two-dimensional computed tomography and image deblurring show consistent gains in reconstruction quality and sharper recovery of fine details.

</details>


### [215] [FEVessel: Mesh-Independent Analysis of 3D Pressure Vessels with the Label-Free Pretrained Finite Element Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17318)
*Yipin Sun,Yizheng Wang,Yuzhou Lin,Baiyang Zheng,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出FEVessel，一种将预训练有限元方法（PFEM）适配到三维压力容器的无标签神经网络算子，该算子以总势能而非FEM标签进行预训练，能泛化多种材料/几何/边界条件，在退化网格上误差低于3.66%，且通过热启动迭代求解器实现9.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元方法（FEM）在压力容器分析中无法摊销重复求解成本，且在退化网格上失效；而神经算子需要FEM生成标签数据，形成循环依赖。因此，需要一种无需标签、能处理退化网格且能复用的求解方法。

Method: FEVessel将每个压力容器编码为包含坐标、材料和载荷通道的点云，基于总势能（而非FEM标签）预训练Transolver算子，并用其预测热启动迭代求解器。训练无需任何标签数据。

Result: 相对位移误差1.35%，应变误差2.07%（比监督式Fourier神经算子低4.7倍）；热启动将代数多重网格迭代从195次降至18次，端到端加速9.2倍；在退化网格上误差低于3.66%；可跨网格分辨率迁移，在30%点密度下保持约3%误差。

Conclusion: FEVessel是首个针对工业相关三维压力容器在退化网格上进行网格无关求解的系统性研究。由于训练无需标签，它能在FEM无法提供标签的场景下工作，从而消除人工网格修复环节。

Abstract: Pressure vessel analysis in the chemical, nuclear, and new-energy industries requires solving the same elasticity problem across many materials, geometries, and loads, where mesh quality and repeated solving govern both accuracy and cost. The finite element method (FEM) cannot amortise this repeated cost and fails on degenerate meshes, while the neural operators meant to replace it still need labelled data that FEM must generate. This paper proposes FEVessel, an adaptation of the Pretrained Finite Element Method (PFEM) to three-dimensional (3D) pressure vessels, and validates four capabilities across the two limitations above. FEVessel i) encodes each vessel as a point cloud with coordinate, material, and load channels, ii) pretrains a Transolver operator on the total potential energy instead of FEM labels, and iii) warm-starts iterative solvers with its prediction. A single model generalises across material, geometry, and boundary conditions at a $1.35\%$ relative displacement error, and its $2.07\%$ strain error is about $4.7$ times lower than that of a supervised Fourier neural operator ($9.72\%$), whose structured grid cannot preserve the through-thickness strain. Its warm start cuts algebraic multigrid iterations from $195$ to $18$, a $9.2\times$ end-to-end wall-clock speedup at the $10^{-3}$ engineering tolerance. The model transfers across mesh resolutions without retraining, holding about $3\%$ error at only $30\%$ of the training point density. On inverted and sliver meshes where FEM fails, the error remains below $3.66\%$. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of mesh-independent solution on industrially relevant 3D pressure vessels with degenerate meshes. Because training needs no labels, FEVessel works exactly where FEM cannot supply any, removing manual mesh repair from the analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [216] [A posteriori error estimates for parabolic PDEs on evolving surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17416)
*Michael Lantelme*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对封闭演化曲面上的抛物线型偏微分方程，推导了基于残差的后验误差估计，证明了所提误差指示子的可靠性和效率，并将静态曲面的自适应方法扩展至演化曲面的粗化处理，数值实验验证了误差渐近行为及细化/粗化策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对静态曲面上抛物型PDE的自适应方法无法直接应用于演化曲面，且演化曲面上后验误差估计的可靠性与效率尚未得到严格证明，因此需要建立适用于演化曲面的误差指示子理论并扩展自适应策略以支持非平凡粗化操作。

Method: 基于残差的后验误差估计方法，推导全局上界（可靠性）与空间全局/时间局部下界（效率）的误差指示子，并将静态曲面的自适应细化/粗化方法推广至演化曲面场景。

Result: 证明了所提误差指示子的可靠性与效率，数值实验展示了误差的渐近行为以及细化与粗化策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于残差的后验误差估计方法为演化曲面上抛物型PDE的自适应计算提供了理论基础，其误差指示子兼具可靠性与效率，扩展后的自适应方法能够有效处理演化曲面上的网格粗化问题。

Abstract: We derive residual-based a posteriori error estimates for parabolic surface PDEs on closed evolving surfaces. The main contribution is to prove efficiency and reliability for the proposed error indicator, which bounds the error quantities globally from above and globally in space and locally in time from below. We extend methods for adaptivity for parabolic PDEs on stationary surfaces to allow for non-trivial coarsening on evolving surfaces. Multiple numerical experiments are given, which illustrate the asymptotic behaviour of the error and effectiveness of the refinement and coarsening.

</details>


### [217] [An operator-splitting algorithm for the hypergraph $p$-Laplacian with applications to missing data recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17606)
*Kehan Shi,Jin Liu,Martin Burger*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对超图p-Laplacian正则化的算子分裂算法，通过高斯-赛德尔方式逐超边处理子问题，并利用ADMM克服非光滑性，在缺失数据恢复（图像稀疏修复和半监督学习）任务上比现有方法更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图p-Laplacian正则化是数据分析中的重要模型，但面临目标函数非光滑、规模大的挑战，现有方法效率不足，亟需更高效的求解算法。

Method: 采用算子分裂框架，以高斯-赛德尔方式逐超边处理子问题；每个子问题视为超边上的广义图Lipschitz学习，引入辅助变量克服非光滑性，并用单步ADMM求解，形成顺序近端ADMM更新算法。

Result: 在图像稀疏修复和半监督学习等缺失数据恢复问题上，所提算法比现有方法更快。

Conclusion: 提出的算子分裂ADMM算法能高效求解超图p-Laplacian正则化问题，收敛性有理论保证，实验验证了速度优势。

Abstract: Hypergraph $p$-Laplacian regularization is a fundamental model in data analysis with successful applications in various tasks. It aims to minimize a nonsmooth and typically large-scale objective function defined as the sum of the $p$-th powers of the Lipschitz regularization over hyperedges. In this paper, we propose an operator-splitting algorithm for the hypergraph $p$-Laplacian that allows us to handle hyperedges separately in a Gauss-Seidel fashion. Each subproblem can be viewed as a generalized graph Lipschitz learning on a hyperedge, for which we introduce an auxiliary variable to overcome the nonsmoothness and solve it with one step of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). The resulting algorithm performs proximal ADMM updates sequentially over the hyperedges, and its convergence is proven. We test the algorithm on missing data recovery problems, including image sparse inpainting and semi-supervised learning, to demonstrate that it is faster than existing methods.

</details>


### [218] [A domain decomposition online-learning-enhanced nonlinear elimination preconditioner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17723)
*Pai Zhang,Linyan Gu,Li Luo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线学习增强的非线性消除预条件子，通过在线收集残差快照并训练无监督提取模型（PCA或自编码器）从残差主导结构中识别坏子集，替代传统依赖原始残差的阈值方法。在并行区域分解框架下，各子域独立训练局部提取模型，用于指导非线性消除过程。在高达Re=10,000的方腔驱动流数值实验中，该方法生成的坏子集更可靠、更连贯，对参数鲁棒，且收敛性优于基线NE预条件子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性消除（NE）预条件中，坏子集的识别依赖问题特定的物理信息或用户调参的阈值直接作用于原始非线性残差。然而在停滞区域附近，原始残差含有不规则的振荡结构，导致所选坏子集对阈值参数高度敏感，缺乏鲁棒性和可靠性。

Method: 提出在线学习增强的NE预条件子：在Newton求解的停滞阶段在线收集残差快照，训练无监督提取模型（线性PCA或非线性自编码器）以捕获主非线性不平衡成分，利用残差重构而非原始残差来定义坏子集。同时集成到并行区域分解框架中，各子域独立训练局部提取模型。

Result: 在Re=1,000到10,000的方腔驱动流数值实验中，该方法生成的坏子集更可靠、更连贯，对NE参数和学习参数均具有鲁棒性，收敛性优于基线NE预条件子。

Conclusion: 在线学习增强的NE预条件子通过从残差主导结构中提取信息而非直接操作原始残差，有效解决了传统方法阈值敏感的问题，提升了预条件子的鲁棒性和收敛性能。

Abstract: Nonlinearly preconditioned inexact Newton methods form an effective class of solvers for large-scale nonlinear algebraic systems arising from the discretization of partial differential equations. A central challenge in nonlinear elimination (NE) preconditioning is the reliable identification of the slowly converging components to be eliminated. Existing selection strategies often rely on problem-specific physical information or user-tuned thresholds applied directly to the raw nonlinear residual, which may contain irregular oscillatory structures near stagnation regions, making the selected bad subset highly sensitive to threshold parameters. In this work, we propose an online-learning-enhanced NE preconditioner that identifies the bad subset from the dominant structure of the nonlinear residual rather than from the raw residual itself. Residual snapshots are collected online during the stagnation phase of the current Newton solve, and an unsupervised extraction model is trained to capture the principal nonlinear imbalance. We consider both a linear extractor based on principal component analysis and nonlinear extractors based on autoencoder neural networks. Moreover, we integrate the approach into a parallel domain decomposition framework, which trains a local extraction model independently on each subdomain. The learned residual reconstruction is then used to define the bad subset and guide the nonlinear elimination process. Numerical experiments on lid-driven cavity flows at Reynolds numbers up to 10,000 show that the proposed method produces more reliable and coherent bad subsets, is robust with respect to both NE and learning parameters, and outperforms the baseline NE preconditioner in terms of the convergence.

</details>


### [219] [Pathwise skew-symmetric discretisation for SDEs with superlinear drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17735)
*Yuga Iguchi,Samuel Livingstone,Giorgos Vasdekis,Rui-Yang Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路径wise的偏对称离散化方法，用于非全局Lipschitz漂移的随机微分方程（SDE）的弱逼近，并将其应用于多层蒙特卡洛（MLMC）框架。该方法通过将噪声增量表示为偏正态分布并与驱动布朗增量耦合，实现了强收敛阶1/2，并达到计算复杂度𝒪(ε^{-2}(log(1/ε))^2)。与驯服欧拉格式相比，在强内向漂移区，偏对称方案使错误方向移动的概率趋于零，而驯服欧拉仍存在非平凡概率。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒离散化方法（如驯服欧拉）在处理非全局Lipschitz漂移的SDE时，在强内向漂移区仍会出现以非平凡概率向错误方向移动的问题，且MLMC框架下耦合层级差异的渐近方差较大。需要一种更优的离散化方案以提升MLMC的效率和稳定性。

Method: 1) 提出路径wise偏对称离散化方案，将噪声增量表示为偏正态分布并与驱动布朗增量耦合；2) 在适当条件下证明L^2意义下的强收敛阶1/2；3) 建立MLMC估计器，推导计算复杂度为𝒪(ε^{-2}(log(1/ε))^2)；4) 与驯服欧拉格式进行理论对比分析（错误方向概率、渐近方差）；5) 用一维随机Ginzburg-Landau模型在MLMC设定下比较渐近方差。

Result: 1) 路径wise偏对称方案具有强收敛阶1/2；2) MLMC估计器计算复杂度为𝒪(ε^{-2}(log(1/ε))^2)达到均方误差ε^2；3) 在强内向漂移区，偏对称方案错误方向移动概率趋于零，而驯服欧拉有非平凡概率；4) 在一维随机Ginzburg-Landau模型中，指定了步长范围使得偏对称方案的渐近方差低于驯服欧拉；5) 数值实验验证了强收敛阶及MLMC的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 路径wise偏对称离散化方法在非全局Lipschitz漂移SDE的MLMC模拟中，相比驯服欧拉方案具有更好的鲁棒性和效率，能有效降低错误方向移动概率和渐近方差，是处理超线性漂移SDE的一个有力工具。

Abstract: The skew-symmetric discretisation has recently been proposed as a new robust simulation method for weakly approximating stochastic differential equations (SDEs) with non-globally Lipschitz drift. This work develops a pathwise version of the scheme by representing the noise increment as a skew-normal distribution and coupling it with the driving Brownian increments, thereby enabling its use in the multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) framework. Under suitable conditions, we establish strong convergence of order 1/2 in $L^2$. Subsequently, the associated MLMC estimator is shown to have computational complexity $\mathcal{O} \bigl(\varepsilon^{-2} (\log (1/\varepsilon))^2 \bigr)$ to achieve a mean-squared error $\varepsilon^2$. We then analytically compare the proposed scheme with the tamed Euler scheme, another benchmark for robust discretisation. Under a strong inward-drift regime with the current state being far from the stable region, we show that the probability of moving in the wrong direction tends to vanish in the skew-symmetric scheme, whereas the tamed Euler scheme makes such moves with a non-trivial probability. Furthermore, in the MLMC setting, employing a one-dimensional stochastic Ginzburg-Landau model, we specify the range of step sizes for which the asymptotic variance of the coupled level difference obtained via the pathwise skew-symmetric scheme is lower than that obtained via the tamed Euler scheme. Numerical experiments on several model examples support the theoretical rate of strong convergence and demonstrate the stability and effectiveness of the resulting MLMC in the superlinear drift setting.

</details>


### [220] [Field-of-values analysis of augmented Krylov methods for matrix $\varphi$-function actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17819)
*Xiaobo Liu,Marcel Schweitzer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了用于矩阵φ-函数作用之线性组合的Krylov子空间方法，从Al-Mohy和Liu提出的块三角公式化视角出发，揭示了两种方法的联系，并将更优的收敛界从后者转移到前者，从而更好地解释了实际观测到的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如KIOPS）使用的增广方法中，较大矩阵的数值域随操作向量增长，导致基于数值域多项式的收敛估计过于悲观，无法解释算法良好的实际性能。

Method: 从Al-Mohy和Liu的块三角公式化视角重新审视Krylov子空间方法，详细推导两种方法的联系，将数值域独立于操作向量的收敛界转移到原增广方法上。

Result: 揭示了两种方法之间的内在联系，成功将更优的收敛界从块三角公式化方法转移到原增广方法，从而更好地解释了原方法实际观测到的良好性能。

Conclusion: 该收敛界转移机制为增广Krylov子空间方法的优异实践性能提供了理论依据，弥合了此前理论与实际表现之间的差距。

Abstract: We revisit established Krylov subspace methods for linear combinations of matrix $\varphi$-function actions from the viewpoint of the block triangular formulation of Al-Mohy and Liu [SIAM J. Sci. Comput., 48 (2026), pp. A726--A747]. In algorithms such as KIOPS [J. Comput. Phys., 372 (2018), pp. 236--255], one uses an augmentation approach based on evaluating the exponential of a slightly larger matrix that contains the operant vectors in its off-diagonal block, and its field of values may therefore grow substantially with these vectors. Typical convergence estimates for Krylov subspace methods result from bounding the error of polynomial approximations for the exponential on the field of values, so that only very pessimistic convergence estimates are available for these methods, in spite of their good practical performance. In contrast, the larger block formulation established by Al-Mohy and Liu involves an operator whose field of values is independent of the operant vectors, leading to more favorable convergence bounds. We work out the details of how these two approaches are connected to each other, which allows us to transfer the convergence bounds from the latter to the former, thus better explaining the observed performance.

</details>


### [221] [Cubature from rational approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17851)
*Gentian Zavalani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Cauchy-Green恒等式和AAA算法（有理逼近）的数值构造方法，用于计算解析函数在具有可求长Jordan边界的平面域上的面积分，将面积分转化为带自由极点的围道积分，极点和残差分别成为积分节点和权重。该方法可恢复圆盘均值法则、椭圆焦点线段法则以及求积域的精确有限求积恒等式，并能从边界数据计算对数和Cauchy体积势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值积分方法（如求积域理论）在处理一般平面域上的解析函数面积分时存在局限性，需要一种通用的、高精度的数值构造框架，能够自动生成积分节点和权重，并具有严格的误差控制。

Method: 基于Cauchy-Green恒等式，选择权函数w的\$\bar\partial\$-反导数W，将面积分转化为围道积分；利用AAA算法用带自由极点的有理函数逼近W的边界值；极点落在域内者成为积分节点，对应残差成为权重；边界残差提供后验误差估计。

Result: 数值实验恢复圆盘均值法则和椭圆焦点线段法则至机器精度；再现求积域的精确有限求积恒等式（含分离和汇合节点）；仅从边界数据即可计算对数和Cauchy体积势；观察到的收敛率在正方形区域上呈根指数收敛。

Conclusion: 该方法将面积分问题归约为边界逼近问题，通过有理逼近自动产生最优节点和权重，具有双重解释（数值求积和有理插值），且求积节点可视为势理论中"母体"（mother bodies）的解析骨架。

Abstract: We present a numerical construction of cubature rules for area integrals of analytic functions over planar domains with rectifiable Jordan boundary. The starting point is the Cauchy--Green identity. Given a weight $w$, we choose a $\bar\partial$-antiderivative $W$ and reduce the area integral to a contour integral involving the boundary values of $W$. These values are then approximated by a rational function with free poles, computed by the AAA algorithm. The poles inside the domain become cubature nodes, the corresponding residues become weights, and the boundary residual controls the error through an a posteriori estimate, rigorous once the continuous boundary residual is bounded. The same rule admits a dual reading, as the exact integral of a rational interpolant to the integrand, the area analogue of the one-dimensional interpolatory viewpoint. The numerical examples recover the disk mean-value rule and the focal-segment rule of the ellipse to machine precision, reproduce the exact finite quadrature identities of quadrature domains with both separated and confluent nodes, and evaluate logarithmic and Cauchy volume potentials from boundary data alone. The interior poles trace analytic skeletons that we identify tentatively with the mother bodies of potential theory, along with image points that appear without being imposed; for the square the observed convergence is root-exponential.

</details>


### [222] [Hybrid-Dimensional Biot Problem with an Optimization Based Domain Decomposition Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17885)
*Francesca Marcon,Stefano Scialò*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对裂缝性多孔介质中流固耦合问题的数值方法，采用混合维度建模，结合XFEM处理非协调网格上的位移和压力不连续性，使用拉格朗日乘子和固定应力分裂方案进行求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决裂缝性多孔介质中流固耦合问题，传统方法要求网格与裂缝完全匹配导致网格生成困难，需要一种允许网格任意穿越裂缝的数值方法。

Method: 1) 采用混合维度域建模；2) 3D网格元素可任意与裂缝相交；3) 使用XFEM在非协调网格上模拟位移不连续；4) 通过拉格朗日乘子构造鞍点问题施加位移连续性；5) XFEM处理压力不连续性；6) 基于优化的域分解策略求解混合维度压力问题；7) 固定应力分裂方案解耦流固子问题；8) CG法求解压力问题。

Result: 固定应力分裂方案与CG求解器的组合对该类问题非常有效。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效处理非协调网格下裂缝性多孔介质的流固耦合问题。

Abstract: The present work proposes a numerical approach for solving coupled flow and mechanics problems in fractured porous media, represented as mixed-dimensional domains. In this formulation, the elements of the 3D mesh are allowed to arbitrarily intersect the fractures. Displacements are discontinuous across fractures through the use of the eXtended Finite Element Method (XFEM) on the 3D mesh. The mechanical problem is formulated as a saddle-point problem, in which Lagrange multipliers are used to enforce displacement continuity across the fractures. The resulting Lagrange multipliers represent the stress field acting on the fracture surfaces. Likewise, the pressure field is allowed to be discontinuous across fractures through the XFEM formulation on the non-conforming mesh and is computed using an optimization-based domain decomposition strategy specifically designed for mixed-dimensional problems. The fixed-stress splitting scheme is employed to decouple the flow and mechanics subproblems, while the mixed-dimensional pressure problem is solved at each fixed-stress iteration using the Conjugate Gradient (CG) method. The combination of the fixed-stress scheme and the CG solver proves to be highly effective for this class of problems.

</details>


### [223] [A Direct Approach to Hermite Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17998)
*Simon Bossoney,Marc Troyanov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族满足Hermite插值自然对偶关系的多项式（类似经典Lagrange插值多项式），给出了任意重数Hermite插值的显式闭式公式，无需使用差商、递归修正或辅助Bezout恒等式。同时详细阐述了Hermite基于积分公式的原始方法，该方法用于推导插值多项式并估计全纯数据下的插值误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Hermite插值方法通常依赖差商、递归修正或Bezout恒等式等复杂工具，缺乏简洁的闭式表达。本文旨在构造一族满足自然对偶关系的多项式，从而直接给出Hermite插值的显式公式。

Method: 引入一族新多项式，使其满足Hermite插值中的自然对偶关系（类似于Lagrange插值基函数）。基于这些多项式直接构造任意重数Hermite插值的闭式解。此外，系统阐述了Hermite本人基于积分公式的原始推导方法。

Result: 成功得到了任意重数Hermite插值的显式闭式公式，避免了传统方法中的差商、递归修正等步骤。同时完整还原了Hermite的积分方法及其在误差估计中的应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的多项式族为Hermite插值提供了一种简洁、直接的构造框架，同时Hermite原始积分方法在全纯数据误差估计中仍具有理论价值。

Abstract: We introduce a family of polynomials satisfying the natural duality relations for Hermite interpolation, analogous to the classical Lagrange interpolation polynomials. They yield an explicit closed formula for the Hermite interpolant with arbitrary multiplicities, without recourse to divided differences, recursive corrections, or auxiliary Bézout identities. We also give a detailed account of Hermite's original approach to the problem, based on an integral formula, which he used both to derive the interpolating polynomial and to estimate the interpolation error for holomorphic data.

</details>


### [224] [Uniform-in-time rational approximation of the matrix exponential with real poles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18018)
*Stefan Güttel,Shuai Shao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种构建具有共享实极点的有理函数族的新方法，用于在正时间区间内近似逼近指数函数 exp(-tz)（z≥0）。第一种方法处理所有实极点合并为单一点的情况，通过选择合适的权重函数导出渐近最优极点位置的闭合公式。第二种方法处理所有实极点互不相同的情况，利用Zolotarev最优有理函数构造，提出了一种高效获取近最优极点的简单算法。分析了浮点算术中评估有理矩阵函数的数值稳定性，通过控制部分分式可能产生的病态增长，获得了可靠且高度可并行的指数传播子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高效、稳定且可并行的指数传播子，用于数值求解涉及矩阵指数的问题。

Method: 方法一：所有实极点合并为单一点，通过权重函数导出渐近最优极点位置的闭合公式。方法二：所有实极点互不相同，利用Zolotarev最优有理函数构造，提出近最优极点的高效算法。

Result: 获得了两种构建近最优有理逼近函数族的方法，并分析了数值稳定性，通过控制部分分式病态增长得到可靠且高度可并行的指数传播子。

Conclusion: 所提出的两类有理逼近方法能有效构造近最优的指数函数逼近，且在浮点算术下具有数值稳定性和并行化优势。

Abstract: We propose two new approaches for constructing families of rational functions with shared real poles that nearly uniformly approximate the functions $\exp(-tz)$ for $z\geq 0$ and $t$ in a positive time interval. The first result concerns the case where all real poles coalesce into a single point. With an appropriate choice of a weight function we are able to derive a closed formula for the asymptotically optimal location of such a pole. We then discuss the more general case where all real poles are distinct. Using Zolotarev's construction of certain optimal rational functions, we present a simple algorithm to derive nearly optimal poles efficiently. We analyze the stability of the numerical evaluation of the resulting rational matrix functions in floating-point arithmetic. By controlling the growth of potential ill-conditioning arising from partial fractions, reliable and highly parallelizable exponential propagators are obtained.

</details>


### [225] [Krasnosel'skii-Mann iterations beyond asymptotics: a combinatorial analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18121)
*Mario Bravo,Roberto Cominetti*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了巴拿赫空间中压缩映射与非膨胀映射的Krasnosel'skii-Mann不动点迭代，通过将其与Z^2上的马尔可夫链建立联系，并利用格路计数组合学方法，导出了迭代距离的显式估计以及不动点残差的非渐近误差界。当压缩参数趋近于1时，这些界光滑地恢复为非膨胀映射的已知估计。基于此，进一步推导了非精确Krasnosel'skii-Mann迭代的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Krasnosel'skii-Mann迭代在凸优化、单调包含、马尔可夫决策过程、非线性PDE松弛方法等领域广泛应用，但缺乏非渐近且精确的误差估计，特别是压缩参数趋近1时的过渡行为尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 将Krasnosel'skii-Mann迭代与Z^2上的马尔可夫链建立联系，利用枚举组合学中的格路计数方法进行分析，得到显式的迭代距离估计和非渐近误差界，并考察压缩参数趋近1时的极限行为。

Result: 得到了压缩映射下迭代距离的显式估计和非渐近残差误差界；这些界在压缩参数趋近1时光滑地恢复为非膨胀映射的已知估计；进一步推广到非精确迭代情形。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种全新的组合学视角来分析Krasnosel'skii-Mann迭代，统一了压缩映射和非膨胀映射的误差分析，并为非精确迭代提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We revisit the classical Krasnosel'skii-Mann fixed point iteration for contractions and nonexpansive maps in general normed spaces. This iteration is ubiquitous across a wide range of areas, including convex optimization, monotone inclusions, Markov decision processes, under-relaxed methods for nonlinear PDEs, and more. Drawing on a remarkable connection with a Markov chain on $\mathbb{Z}^2$, and using counting arguments from enumerative combinatorics of lattice paths, we derive explicit estimates for the distance between iterates, as well as non-asymptotic error bounds for the fixed point residuals. As the contraction parameter approaches one, these bounds smoothly recover the known estimates for nonexpansive maps. Building upon these estimates, we further derive error bounds for inexact Krasnosel'skii-Mann iterations.

</details>
